      var d = String(el.value || '').replace(/\D/g, '');
      el.value = d ? d.replace(/\B(?=(\d{3})+(?!\d))/g, '.') : '';
    }
    function isKgbForm() {
      var v = (document.getElementById('page_input_jenis_kegiatan') || {}).value || '';
      return /^kenaikan gaji berkala/i.test((v.split('|')[1] || '').trim());
    }
    function applyKgbMode() {
      var kgb = isKgbForm();
      var t = function(id, on) { var e = document.getElementById(id); if (e) e.classList.toggle('d-none', !on); };
      t('wrapKgbJumlah', kgb);
      // KGB: bagian Identitas disembunyikan; pegawai dipilih lewat "Pilih Pegawai dari Database" (nama & NIP tetap terisi otomatis)
      t('wrapIdentitasTitle', !kgb); t('wrapIdentitasRow', !kgb);
      ['page_input_nama', 'page_input_nip'].forEach(function(id) { var e = document.getElementById(id); if (e) e.required = !kgb; });
      if (kgb) {
        ['wrapPangkatRow', 'wrapJabatanRow', 'wrapKelasJabatan', 'wrapUsulan', 'wrapInstansiRow', 'wrapUnitBaru', 'blokTugasTambahan'].forEach(function(id) { t(id, false); });
        ['page_input_pangkat_baru', 'page_input_jabatan_baru', 'page_input_kelas_jabatan', 'page_input_kelas_jabatan_lama', 'page_input_nomor_usulan', 'page_input_tanggal_usulan', 'page_input_tt_lama', 'page_input_tt_baru', 'page_input_unit_kerja_baru'].forEach(function(id) { var e = document.getElementById(id); if (e) e.value = ''; });
        var ul = document.getElementById('wrapUnitLama'); if (ul) ul.className = 'col-md-12';
        var set = function(id, h) { var e = document.getElementById(id); if (e) e.innerHTML = h; };
        set('lblUnitLama', 'Unit Kerja / Unor');
        set('lblNoSK', 'Nomor SK KGB'); set('lblTglSK', 'Tanggal SK KGB'); set('lblTMT', 'TMT SK KGB <span class="text-danger">*</span>');
      } else {
        ['wrapPangkatRow', 'wrapJabatanRow', 'wrapUnitBaru', 'wrapInstansiRow'].forEach(function(id) { t(id, true); });
        var ul2 = document.getElementById('wrapUnitLama'); if (ul2) ul2.className = 'col-md-6';
        var l = document.getElementById('lblUnitLama'); if (l) l.innerHTML = 'Unit Kerja / Unor Asal - Perangkat Daerah Lama';
        var j = document.getElementById('page_input_jumlah_kgb'); if (j) j.value = '';
      }
    }

    // ===== HITUNG OTOMATIS MASA PLT/PLH DARI TMT (3 bulan, 6 bulan, 1 tahun) =====
    function updatePltCalc() {
      var box = document.getElementById('pltCalcBox'), rows = document.getElementById('pltCalcRows');
      if (!box || !rows) return;
      var v = (document.getElementById('page_input_jenis_kegiatan') || {}).value || '';
      var isPlt = /pelaksana tugas/i.test(v.split('|')[1] || '');
      var tmt = (document.getElementById('page_input_tmt') || {}).value || '';
      if (!isPlt || !tmt) { box.classList.add('d-none'); return; }
      var fmt = function(d) { return new Date(d + 'T00:00:00').toLocaleDateString('id-ID', { day: 'numeric', month: 'long', year: 'numeric' }); };
      var last = function(n) { return addMonthsToDate(tmt, n); }; // sama dengan perhitungan 'Batas 3 Bulan' di Pemantauan PLT
      var items = [['3 Bulan', 3], ['6 Bulan', 6], ['1 Tahun', 12]];
      rows.innerHTML = items.map(function(it) {
        return '<div class="col-md-4"><div class="bg-white border rounded-3 py-2 px-2"><div class="small text-muted">' + it[0] + '</div><div class="fw-bold text-dark">' + fmt(last(it[1])) + '</div><div class="small text-muted">TMT ' + fmt(tmt) + ' s.d.</div></div></div>';
      }).join('');
      box.classList.remove('d-none');
    }

    // ===== INPUT JABATAN TUGAS TAMBAHAN (KEPALA SEKOLAH / PUSKESMAS) - diinput sendiri =====
    function kelasOnlyLine(x) {
      if (!x || (!x.kelasJabatanLama && !x.kelasJabatan)) return '';
      return '<span class="d-block text-muted">Kelas: ' + esc(x.kelasJabatanLama || '-') + ' &rarr; ' + esc(x.kelasJabatan || '-') + '</span>';
    }
    function ttLine(x) {
      var kl = '';
      if (x && (x.kelasJabatanLama || x.kelasJabatan)) kl = '<div class="small mt-1"><span class="text-muted">Kelas Jabatan:</span> ' + esc(x.kelasJabatanLama || '-') + ' &rarr; <strong class="text-dark">' + esc(x.kelasJabatan || '-') + '</strong></div>';
      if (!x || (!x.tugasTambahanLama && !x.tugasTambahanBaru)) return kl;
      return kl + '<div class="small mt-1 pt-1 border-top"><span class="text-muted">Tugas Tambahan:</span> ' + esc(x.tugasTambahanLama || '-') + ' &rarr; <strong class="text-dark">' + esc(x.tugasTambahanBaru || '-') + '</strong></div>';
    }

    function ttInfo() {
      var v = (document.getElementById('page_input_jenis_kegiatan') || {}).value || '';
      var nama = (v.split('|')[1] || '').toLowerCase();
      var tipe = nama.indexOf('kepala sekolah') >= 0 ? 'sekolah' : (nama.indexOf('kepala puskesmas') >= 0 ? 'puskesmas' : '');
      return tipe ? { tipe: tipe } : null;
    }
    // Pemberhentian tugas tambahan: Jabatan Tugas Tambahan Baru otomatis sama dengan Jabatan Baru
    function ttIsBerhenti() {
      var v = (document.getElementById('page_input_jenis_kegiatan') || {}).value || '';
      return /^pemberhentian/i.test((v.split('|')[1] || '').trim());
    }
    function ttSetBaruMirror(on) {
      var el = document.getElementById('page_input_tt_baru'); if (!el) return;
      el.readOnly = !!on;
      el.classList.toggle('bg-light', !!on);
      if (on) { var jb = document.getElementById('page_input_jabatan_baru'); el.value = jb ? jb.value : ''; }
    }
    document.addEventListener('input', function(e) {
      if (e.target && e.target.id === 'page_input_jabatan_baru' && ttInfo() && ttIsBerhenti()) {
        document.getElementById('page_input_tt_baru').value = e.target.value;
      }
    });

    function onJenisTugasTambahan() {
      var info = ttInfo();
      ['wrapJenisJabatan', 'wrapKelasJabatan'].forEach(function(id) { var w = document.getElementById(id); if (w) w.classList.toggle('d-none', !!info); });
      var pg = document.getElementById('page_input_pangkat_gol'); if (pg && pg.parentElement) pg.parentElement.className = info ? 'col-md-12' : 'col-md-6';
      if (info) {
        ['page_input_jenis_jabatan', 'page_input_kelas_jabatan', 'page_input_kelas_jabatan_lama'].forEach(function(id) { var e = document.getElementById(id); if (e) e.value = ''; });
      }
      var box = document.getElementById('blokTugasTambahan');
      if (!box) return;
      if (!info) { box.classList.add('d-none'); ttSetBaruMirror(false); return; }
      box.classList.remove('d-none');
      ttSetBaruMirror(ttIsBerhenti());
      document.getElementById('ttJudul').textContent = '– ' + (info.tipe === 'sekolah' ? 'Kepala Sekolah' : 'Kepala Puskesmas');
    }

    function resetStandaloneInputForm() {
      document.getElementById('standaloneInputForm').reset();
      document.getElementById('page_input_pegawai_id').value = '';
      document.getElementById('page_input_kegiatan_id').value = '';
      document.getElementById('page_input_file_data').value = '';
      var today = new Date().toISOString().substring(0, 10);
      document.getElementById('page_input_tanggal').value = today;
      document.getElementById('page_input_tmt').value = today;
      try { onJenisTugasTambahan(); applyKenaikanPangkatMode(); applyKgbMode(); } catch (e) {}
      var _ips = document.getElementById('inputPegSearch'); if (_ips) _ips.value = '';
      try { updatePltCalc(); } catch (e) {}
      document.getElementById('page_input_instansi_asal').value = 'Pemerintah Kabupaten Pasuruan';
      document.getElementById('page_input_instansi_tujuan').value = 'Pemerintah Kabupaten Pasuruan';
      var infoEl = document.getElementById('fileUploadInfo');
      if (infoEl) infoEl.innerHTML = 'Format didukung: PDF, PNG, JPG. Maksimal 1 MB (1.024 KB).';
      updateInputTimeInfo();
    }

    function submitStandaloneInputForm() {
      var kegID = document.getElementById('page_input_kegiatan_id').value;
      var pegID = document.getElementById('page_input_pegawai_id').value;
      var jenisVal = document.getElementById('page_input_jenis_kegiatan').value;
      var tglKeg = document.getElementById('page_input_tanggal').value;
      var tmtVal = document.getElementById('page_input_tmt').value;

      var namaVal = document.getElementById('page_input_nama').value.trim();
      var nipVal = document.getElementById('page_input_nip').value.trim();

      if (!jenisVal || !tglKeg || !tmtVal) return Swal.fire('Peringatan', 'Nama Jenis Kegiatan, Tanggal, & TMT wajib diisi!', 'warning');
      if (!namaVal || !nipVal) return Swal.fire('Peringatan', isKgbForm() ? 'Pilih pegawai terlebih dahulu pada kolom "Pilih Pegawai dari Database".' : 'Nama Lengkap & NIP/NIPPPK wajib diisi!', 'warning');

      if (/^kenaikan gaji berkala/i.test((jenisVal.split('|')[1] || '').trim()) && (!namaVal || !nipVal)) return Swal.fire('Peringatan', 'Pilih pegawai terlebih dahulu pada kolom "Pilih Pegawai dari Database".', 'warning');
      if (/^kenaikan gaji berkala/i.test((jenisVal.split('|')[1] || '').trim()) && !(document.getElementById('page_input_jumlah_kgb').value || '').replace(/\D/g, '')) return Swal.fire('Peringatan', 'Jumlah pegawai pada SK KGB wajib diisi!', 'warning');
      var parts = jenisVal.split('|');
      var jenisID = parts[0];
      var jenisNama = parts[1];

      var peg = dbPegawai.find(function(x){ return x.pegawaiID === pegID; });

      var payload = {
        kegiatanID: kegID,
        pegawaiID: pegID,
        nama: namaVal,
        statusPegawai: peg ? peg.statusPegawai : (nipVal.length === 18 ? 'PNS' : 'PPPK'),
        nip: nipVal,
        jenisKegiatanID: jenisID,
        namaJenisKegiatan: jenisNama,
        tanggalKegiatan: tglKeg,
        pangkatGol: document.getElementById('page_input_pangkat_gol').value.trim(),
        pangkatGolBaru: (document.getElementById('page_input_pangkat_baru').value || '').trim(),
        jenisJabatan: document.getElementById('page_input_jenis_jabatan').value.trim(),
        kelasJabatan: document.getElementById('page_input_kelas_jabatan').value.trim(),
        kelasJabatanLama: document.getElementById('page_input_kelas_jabatan_lama').value.trim(),
        jabatanLama: document.getElementById('page_input_jabatan_lama').value.trim(),
        jabatanBaru: document.getElementById('page_input_jabatan_baru').value.trim(),
        tugasTambahanLama: (document.getElementById('page_input_tt_lama').value || '').trim(),
        tugasTambahanBaru: (document.getElementById('page_input_tt_baru').value || '').trim(),
        unitKerjaLama: document.getElementById('page_input_unit_kerja_lama').value.trim(),
        unitKerjaBaru: isKgbForm() ? document.getElementById('page_input_unit_kerja_lama').value.trim() : document.getElementById('page_input_unit_kerja_baru').value.trim(),
        instansiAsal: document.getElementById('page_input_instansi_asal').value.trim() || 'Pemerintah Kabupaten Pasuruan',
        instansiTujuan: document.getElementById('page_input_instansi_tujuan').value.trim() || 'Pemerintah Kabupaten Pasuruan',
        jumlahKGB: (document.getElementById('page_input_jumlah_kgb').value || '').replace(/\D/g, ''),
        nomorUsulan: document.getElementById('page_input_nomor_usulan').value.trim(),
        tanggalUsulan: document.getElementById('page_input_tanggal_usulan').value,
        nomorSK: document.getElementById('page_input_nomor_sk').value.trim(),
        tanggalSK: document.getElementById('page_input_tanggal_sk').value,
        tmt: tmtVal,
        fileData: document.getElementById('page_input_file_data').value,
        jumlah: 1,
        keterangan: document.getElementById('page_input_keterangan').value.trim()
      };

      var tempId = kegID || ('KEG-TEMP-' + Date.now());
      var tempItem = Object.assign({}, payload, { kegiatanID: tempId, _isSyncing: true });
      if (!kegID) tempItem.createdAt = nowWIB()
      if (!kegID && currentUser) { tempItem.inputUsername = currentUser.username || ''; tempItem.inputNama = currentUser.fullname || ''; }
      var backupData = JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(dbKegiatan));

      if (kegID) {
        var idx = dbKegiatan.findIndex(function(x){ return x.kegiatanID === kegID; });
        if (idx !== -1) dbKegiatan[idx] = Object.assign({}, dbKegiatan[idx], tempItem);
      } else {
        dbKegiatan.unshift(tempItem);
      }

      Swal.fire({ icon: 'info', title: 'Menyimpan data kepegawaian...', toast: true, position: 'bottom-end', showConfirmButton: false, timer: 2000 });

      if (typeof google !== 'undefined') {
        gasRun('saveDataKegiatan', payload)
          .then(function(res) {
            if (res.success) {
              resetStandaloneInputForm();
              Swal.fire({ icon: 'success', title: 'Data Kepegawaian Berhasil Disimpan', toast: true, position: 'top-end', showConfirmButton: false, timer: 2500 });
              // segarkan daftar di latar belakang (tidak menahan layar & tidak berebut dengan permintaan berikutnya)
              setTimeout(function() { fetchKegiatanData(true); fetchPegawaiData(true); refreshIzin(); }, 1200);
            } else {
              dbKegiatan = backupData;
              Swal.fire('Error', res.message, 'error');
            }
          })
          .catch(function(err) {
            dbKegiatan = backupData;
            Swal.fire('Error', err.message || 'Gagal menyimpan', 'error');
          });
      } else {
        setTimeout(function() {
          var simIdx = dbKegiatan.findIndex(function(x){ return x.kegiatanID === tempId; });
          if (simIdx !== -1) delete dbKegiatan[simIdx]._isSyncing;
          Swal.fire({ icon: 'success', title: 'Tersimpan (Simulasi)', text: 'Data kepegawaian berhasil dicatat!', confirmButtonText: 'OK' }).then(function() {
            resetStandaloneInputForm();
          });
        }, 800);
      }
    }

    // ===== PENCARIAN & HALAMAN DATA KEGIATAN =====
    var DATA_PER_PAGE = 10, dataPage = 1;
    var kegKeyMap = new WeakMap();
    function kegKey(x) {
      var k = kegKeyMap.get(x);
      if (k === undefined) {
        k = [x.nama, x.nip, x.namaJenisKegiatan, x.jabatanLama, x.jabatanBaru, x.unitKerjaLama, x.unitKerjaBaru, x.nomorSK, x.tugasTambahanLama, x.tugasTambahanBaru, x.kegiatanID]
          .map(function(v) { return v === null || v === undefined ? '' : String(v); }).join('\u0001').toLowerCase();
        kegKeyMap.set(x, k);
      }
      return k;
    }
    function gotoDataPage(p) { dataPage = p; renderDataKegiatanTable(); var c = document.getElementById('dataTableContainer'); if (c && c.scrollIntoView) c.scrollIntoView({ block: 'start' }); }
    function buildDataPager(total, totalPages) {
      if (total <= 0) return '';
      var from = (dataPage - 1) * DATA_PER_PAGE + 1, to = Math.min(dataPage * DATA_PER_PAGE, total);
      var groupStart = Math.floor((dataPage - 1) / 10) * 10 + 1;
      var groupEnd = Math.min(groupStart + 9, totalPages);
      var h = '<div class="d-flex justify-content-between align-items-center flex-wrap gap-2 p-3 border-top no-print">' +
        '<small class="text-muted">Menampilkan ' + from + '&ndash;' + to + ' dari ' + total + ' data &middot; Halaman ' + dataPage + ' / ' + totalPages + '</small>' +
        '<nav><ul class="pagination pagination-sm mb-0">';
      h += '<li class="page-item' + (dataPage <= 1 ? ' disabled' : '') + '"><a class="page-link" href="#" onclick="gotoDataPage(' + (dataPage - 1) + '); return false;">&laquo; Sebelumnya</a></li>';
      if (groupStart > 1) h += '<li class="page-item"><a class="page-link" href="#" title="10 halaman sebelumnya" onclick="gotoDataPage(' + (groupStart - 1) + '); return false;">&hellip;</a></li>';
      for (var p = groupStart; p <= groupEnd; p++) {
        h += '<li class="page-item' + (p === dataPage ? ' active' : '') + '"><a class="page-link" href="#" onclick="gotoDataPage(' + p + '); return false;">' + p + '</a></li>';
      }
      if (groupEnd < totalPages) h += '<li class="page-item"><a class="page-link" href="#" title="Halaman berikutnya" onclick="gotoDataPage(' + (groupEnd + 1) + '); return false;">&hellip;</a></li>';
      h += '<li class="page-item' + (dataPage >= totalPages ? ' disabled' : '') + '"><a class="page-link" href="#" onclick="gotoDataPage(' + (dataPage + 1) + '); return false;">Berikutnya &raquo;</a></li>';
      return h + '</ul></nav></div>';
    }

    function renderDataKegiatanTable() {
      var container = document.getElementById('dataTableContainer');
      if (!container) return;

      var search = String(document.getElementById('searchDataInput') ? document.getElementById('searchDataInput').value : '').trim().toLowerCase();
      var filterJenis = document.getElementById('filterJenisKegiatan') ? document.getElementById('filterJenisKegiatan').value : '';

      var filtered = dbKegiatan.filter(function(x) {
        var matchJenis = filterJenis === '' || String(x.namaJenisKegiatan || '').indexOf(filterJenis) !== -1;
        return matchJenis && (search === '' || kegKey(x).indexOf(search) !== -1);
      });

      if (filtered.length === 0) {
        container.innerHTML = '<div class="text-center py-5"><i class="fa-solid fa-folder-open fa-3x text-muted mb-3 opacity-50"></i><p class="text-muted">Tidak ada data kegiatan ditemukan.</p></div>';
        return;
      }

      var totalFiltered = filtered.length;
      var totalPages = Math.max(1, Math.ceil(totalFiltered / DATA_PER_PAGE));
      if (dataPage > totalPages) dataPage = totalPages;
      if (dataPage < 1) dataPage = 1;
      filtered = filtered.slice((dataPage - 1) * DATA_PER_PAGE, dataPage * DATA_PER_PAGE);
      var html = '<div class="table-responsive"><table class="table table-custom table-hover align-middle mb-0"><thead><tr><th>ID & Tanggal</th><th>Pegawai & NIP</th><th>Jenis Kegiatan Baku</th><th>Jabatan Lama &amp; Unor Lama</th><th>Jabatan Baru &amp; Unor Baru</th><th>Nomor SK, Tgl SK & TMT</th><th>Waktu Input &amp; Diinput Oleh</th><th class="text-end">Aksi</th></tr></thead><tbody>';

      filtered.forEach(function(item) {
        var isSyncing = item._isSyncing === true;
        var fileBtn = item.fileData ? '<button class="btn btn-sm btn-outline-secondary me-1" title="Lihat Berkas" onclick="previewUploadedDoc(\'' + escJs(item.kegiatanID) + '\')"><i class="fa-solid fa-paperclip text-primary"></i></button>' : '';
        var actionCol = isSyncing
          ? '<span class="spinner-border spinner-border-sm text-primary me-1"></span><small class="text-muted fst-italic">Syncing...</small>'
          : fileBtn + '<button class="btn btn-sm btn-light border text-primary me-1" title="Edit" onclick="editKegiatan(\'' + escJs(item.kegiatanID) + '\')"><i class="fa-solid fa-pen-to-square"></i></button>' + '<button class="btn btn-sm btn-light border text-danger" title="Hapus" onclick="deleteKegiatanAction(\'' + escJs(item.kegiatanID) + '\')"><i class="fa-solid fa-trash"></i></button>';

        html += '<tr class="' + (isSyncing ? 'opacity-60' : '') + '">' +
          '<td><div><code class="d-block">' + esc(item.kegiatanID) + '</code><small class="text-muted"><i class="fa-solid fa-calendar-day me-1"></i>' + safeDate(item.tanggalKegiatan) + '</small></div></td>' +
          '<td><div><strong class="text-dark d-block">' + esc(item.nama || '-') + '</strong><small class="text-muted d-block">NIP/NIPPPK: ' + esc(item.nip || '-') + '</small><small class="text-dark">Pangkat/Gol.Ruang: ' + esc(item.pangkatGol || '-') + (item.pangkatGolBaru ? ' &rarr; <strong>' + esc(item.pangkatGolBaru) + '</strong>' : '') + '</small></div></td>' +
          '<td><span class="badge bg-primary-subtle text-primary border px-2.5 py-1 text-wrap text-start" style="max-width:220px;">' + esc(item.namaJenisKegiatan || '-') + '</span></td>' +
          '<td><div class="small"><span class="text-muted">Jabatan:</span> ' + esc(item.jabatanLama || '-') + '</div><div class="small"><span class="text-muted">Unor:</span> ' + esc(item.unitKerjaLama || '-') + '</div>' + (item.kelasJabatanLama ? '<div class="small"><span class="text-muted">Kelas Jabatan:</span> ' + esc(item.kelasJabatanLama) + '</div>' : '') + (item.tugasTambahanLama ? '<div class="small"><span class="text-muted">Tugas Tambahan:</span> ' + esc(item.tugasTambahanLama) + '</div>' : '') + '</td>' +
          '<td><div class="small"><span class="text-muted">Jabatan:</span> <strong class="text-dark">' + esc(item.jabatanBaru || '-') + '</strong></div><div class="small"><span class="text-muted">Unor:</span> <strong class="text-dark">' + esc(item.unitKerjaBaru || '-') + '</strong></div>' + (item.kelasJabatan ? '<div class="small"><span class="text-muted">Kelas Jabatan:</span> ' + esc(item.kelasJabatan) + '</div>' : '') + (item.tugasTambahanBaru ? '<div class="small"><span class="text-muted">Tugas Tambahan:</span> ' + esc(item.tugasTambahanBaru) + '</div>' : '') + '</td>' +
          '<td><div><span class="d-block small text-dark">SK: ' + esc(item.nomorSK || '-') + '</span><small class="d-block text-muted">Tgl SK: ' + safeDate(item.tanggalSK) + '</small><small class="text-primary fw-semibold">TMT: ' + safeDate(item.tmt) + '</small>' + (item.jumlahKGB ? '<small class="d-block text-success fw-semibold">Jumlah Pegawai SK: ' + esc(item.jumlahKGB) + ' orang</small>' : '') + '</div></td>' +
          '<td>' + fmtDateTimeHtml(item.createdAt) + (item.inputNama || item.inputUsername ? '<div class="small mt-1"><i class="fa-solid fa-user-pen text-muted me-1"></i><strong class="text-dark">' + esc(item.inputNama || item.inputUsername) + '</strong>' + (item.inputUsername ? '<br><span class="text-muted">@' + esc(item.inputUsername) + '</span>' : '') + '</div>' : '<div class="small text-muted mt-1">Oleh: -</div>') + '</td>' +
          '<td class="text-end">' + actionCol + '</td>' +
        '</tr>';
      });

      html += '</tbody></table></div>';
      setTimeout(function() { prefetchVisibleLampiran('dataTableContainer'); }, 0);
      html += buildDataPager(totalFiltered, totalPages);
      container.innerHTML = html;
    }

    function renderPltMonitoringView() {
      var container = document.getElementById('pltMonitoringContainer');
      if (!container) return;

      var search = (document.getElementById('searchPltInput')?.value || '').toLowerCase();
      var filterStatus = document.getElementById('filterPltStatus')?.value || '';

      var pltList = dbKegiatan.filter(function(x) {
        if (!x.namaJenisKegiatan) return false;
        var nameLower = x.namaJenisKegiatan.toLowerCase();
        return nameLower.includes('plt') || nameLower.includes('pelaksana tugas');
      });

      var todayStr = new Date().toISOString().substring(0, 10);
      var todayDate = new Date(todayStr);

      var evaluatedPlt = pltList.map(function(item) {
        var tmtDateStr = item.tmt || item.tanggalKegiatan || todayStr;
        var extObj = dbPltExtensions[item.kegiatanID];
        var activeTmt = (extObj && extObj.latestTmt) ? extObj.latestTmt : tmtDateStr;
        var target3Months = addMonthsToDate(activeTmt, 3);
        var targetDate = new Date(target3Months);

        var extCount = (extObj && extObj.history) ? extObj.history.length : 0;
        var baseStatus = (extObj && extObj.status) ? extObj.status : 'AKTIF';

        if (baseStatus !== 'SELESAI' && baseStatus !== 'DIPERPANJANG') {
          if (todayDate >= targetDate) {
            baseStatus = 'MENCAPI_3_BULAN';
          } else {
            baseStatus = 'AKTIF';
          }
        }

        return {
          item: item,
          activeTmt: activeTmt,
          target3Months: target3Months,
          extCount: extCount,
          status: baseStatus
        };
      });

      var filtered = evaluatedPlt.filter(function(p) {
        var matchStatus = filterStatus === '' || p.status === filterStatus;
        var matchSearch = search === '' ||
          (p.item.nama && p.item.nama.toLowerCase().includes(search)) ||
          (p.item.nip && p.item.nip.toLowerCase().includes(search)) ||
          (p.item.jabatanBaru && p.item.jabatanBaru.toLowerCase().includes(search)) ||
          (p.item.nomorSK && p.item.nomorSK.toLowerCase().includes(search));
        return matchStatus && matchSearch;
      });

      var alertCount = evaluatedPlt.filter(function(p) { return p.status === 'MENCAPI_3_BULAN'; }).length;
      var badgeAlertEl = document.getElementById('pltAlertBadgeCount');
      if (badgeAlertEl) badgeAlertEl.innerText = alertCount + ' PLT Mencapai 3 Bulan (Perlu Tindak Lanjut)';

      if (filtered.length === 0) {
        container.innerHTML = '<div class="text-center py-5"><i class="fa-solid fa-clock-rotate-left fa-3x text-muted mb-3 opacity-50"></i><p class="text-muted">Tidak ada data penugasan PLT yang dipantau.</p></div>';
        return;
      }

      var html = '<div class="table-responsive"><table class="table table-custom table-hover align-middle mb-0"><thead><tr><th>Pejabat & NIP</th><th>Jabatan Lama &rarr; Baru</th><th>Unor Lama &rarr; Baru</th><th>No. SK &amp; Tgl SK</th><th>Berkas</th><th>TMT Mulai (Aktif)</th><th>Batas 3 Bulan SK</th><th>Status Pemantauan</th><th class="text-end">Aksi Tindak Lanjut</th></tr></thead><tbody>';

      filtered.forEach(function(obj) {
        var item = obj.item;
        var statusBadge = '';
        if (obj.status === 'MENCAPI_3_BULAN') {
          statusBadge = '<span class="badge bg-danger text-white border px-2.5 py-1 animate-pulse"><i class="fa-solid fa-triangle-exclamation me-1"></i>Mencapai 3 Bulan</span>';
        } else if (obj.status === 'DIPERPANJANG') {
          statusBadge = '<span class="badge bg-success-subtle text-success border px-2.5 py-1"><i class="fa-solid fa-check me-1"></i>Diperpanjang (Ke-' + obj.extCount + ')</span>';
        } else if (obj.status === 'SELESAI') {
          statusBadge = '<span class="badge bg-secondary-subtle text-secondary border px-2.5 py-1">Selesai / Nonaktif</span>';
        } else {
          statusBadge = '<span class="badge bg-primary-subtle text-primary border px-2.5 py-1">Aktif (&lt; 3 Bulan)</span>';
        }

        var actionBtns = '';
        if (obj.status !== 'SELESAI') {
          actionBtns += '<button class="btn btn-sm btn-outline-primary me-1" title="Perpanjang PLT" onclick="openPltPerpanjangModal(\'' + escJs(item.kegiatanID) + '\')"><i class="fa-solid fa-calendar-plus me-1"></i> Perpanjang</button>';
        }
        if (obj.extCount > 0) {
          actionBtns += '<button class="btn btn-sm btn-outline-secondary me-1" title="Riwayat Perpanjangan" onclick="openPltRiwayatModal(\'' + escJs(item.kegiatanID) + '\')"><i class="fa-solid fa-history me-1"></i> (' + obj.extCount + ')</button>';
        }
        if (obj.status !== 'SELESAI') {
          actionBtns += '<button class="btn btn-sm btn-outline-danger" title="Selesaikan / Nonaktifkan" onclick="completePltAssignment(\'' + escJs(item.kegiatanID) + '\')"><i class="fa-solid fa-ban"></i> Selesaikan</button>';
        } else {
          actionBtns += '<span class="small text-muted fst-italic">Selesai</span>';
        }

        html += '<tr>' +
          '<td><div><strong class="text-dark d-block">' + esc(item.nama || '-') + '</strong><small class="text-muted">NIP: ' + esc(item.nip || '-') + '</small></div></td>' +
          '<td>' + '<div class="small"><span class="text-muted">Lama:</span> ' + esc(item.jabatanLama || '-') + '</div><div class="small"><span class="text-muted">Baru:</span> <strong class="text-dark">' + esc(item.jabatanBaru || item.namaJenisKegiatan || '-') + '</strong></div>' + ttLine(item) + '</td>' +
          '<td>' + '<div class="small"><span class="text-muted">Lama:</span> ' + esc(item.unitKerjaLama || '-') + '</div><div class="small"><span class="text-muted">Baru:</span> <strong class="text-dark">' + esc(item.unitKerjaBaru || '-') + '</strong></div>' + '</td>' +
          '<td><small class="text-dark d-block">' + esc(item.nomorSK || '-') + '</small><small class="text-muted">Tgl SK: ' + safeDate(item.tanggalSK) + '</small></td>' +
          '<td>' + (item.fileData ? '<button class="btn btn-sm btn-outline-secondary" title="Lihat Berkas SK" onclick="previewUploadedDoc(\'' + escJs(item.kegiatanID) + '\')"><i class="fa-solid fa-file-pdf me-1"></i>Lihat</button>' : '<small class="text-muted">-</small>') + '</td>' +
          '<td><small class="fw-semibold text-dark">' + safeDate(obj.activeTmt) + '</small></td>' +
          '<td><small class="fw-bold text-danger">' + safeDate(obj.target3Months) + '</small></td>' +
          '<td>' + statusBadge + '</td>' +
          '<td class="text-end">' + actionBtns + '</td>' +
        '</tr>';
      });

      html += '</tbody></table></div>';
      setTimeout(function() { prefetchVisibleLampiran('pltMonitoringContainer'); }, 0);
      container.innerHTML = html;
    }

    function openPltPerpanjangModal(kegiatanID) {
      var item = dbKegiatan.find(function(x){ return x.kegiatanID === kegiatanID; });
      if (!item) return;

      var extObj = dbPltExtensions[kegiatanID];
      var defaultTmt = (extObj && extObj.latestTmt) ? extObj.latestTmt : (item.tmt || item.tanggalKegiatan);

      document.getElementById('plt_perpanjang_id').value = kegiatanID;
      document.getElementById('plt_perpanjang_nama').value = item.nama + ' — ' + (item.jabatanBaru || item.namaJenisKegiatan);
      document.getElementById('plt_perpanjang_nosk').value = '';
      document.getElementById('plt_perpanjang_tmt').value = defaultTmt;
      calcPltPerpanjangEnd(defaultTmt);
      document.getElementById('plt_perpanjang_ket').value = '';

      new bootstrap.Modal(document.getElementById('pltPerpanjangModal')).show();
    }

    function calcPltPerpanjangEnd(tmtVal) {
      if (!tmtVal) return;
      var endVal = addMonthsToDate(tmtVal, 3);
      document.getElementById('plt_perpanjang_end').value = endVal;
    }

    function submitPltPerpanjang() {
      var kegID = document.getElementById('plt_perpanjang_id').value;
      var noSK = document.getElementById('plt_perpanjang_nosk').value.trim();
      var tmtNew = document.getElementById('plt_perpanjang_tmt').value;
      var tmtEnd = document.getElementById('plt_perpanjang_end').value;
      var ket = document.getElementById('plt_perpanjang_ket').value.trim();

      if (!noSK || !tmtNew) return Swal.fire('Peringatan', 'Nomor SK dan Tanggal Mulai Perpanjangan wajib diisi!', 'warning');

      var modalEl = document.getElementById('pltPerpanjangModal');
      var modalInst = bootstrap.Modal.getInstance(modalEl);
      if (modalInst) modalInst.hide();

      if (!dbPltExtensions[kegID]) {
        dbPltExtensions[kegID] = { latestTmt: tmtNew, status: 'DIPERPANJANG', history: [] };
      }

      var extRecord = {
        noSK: noSK,
        tmtMulai: tmtNew,
        tmtBerakhir: tmtEnd,
        keterangan: ket,
        tanggalPerpanjangan: new Date().toISOString().substring(0, 10)
      };

      dbPltExtensions[kegID].history.push(extRecord);
      dbPltExtensions[kegID].latestTmt = tmtNew;
      dbPltExtensions[kegID].status = 'DIPERPANJANG';
      savePltExt(kegID);

      renderPltMonitoringView();
      Swal.fire({ icon: 'success', title: 'Perpanjangan PLT Berhasil!', text: 'Periode pemantauan 3 bulan baru telah diset.', timer: 2000, showConfirmButton: false });
    }

    function completePltAssignment(kegiatanID) {
      Swal.fire({
        title: 'Nonaktifkan / Selesaikan PLT?',
        text: 'Penugasan PLT ini akan dinyatakan selesai dan peringatan 3 bulan dihentikan.',
        icon: 'question',
        showCancelButton: true,
        confirmButtonText: 'Ya, Selesaikan',
        cancelButtonText: 'Batal'
      }).then(function(res) {
        if (!res.isConfirmed) return;
        if (!dbPltExtensions[kegiatanID]) {
          dbPltExtensions[kegiatanID] = { latestTmt: '', status: 'SELESAI', history: [] };
        } else {
          dbPltExtensions[kegiatanID].status = 'SELESAI';
        }
        savePltExt(kegiatanID);
        renderPltMonitoringView();
        Swal.fire({ icon: 'success', title: 'Penugasan PLT Selesai', toast: true, position: 'top-end', showConfirmButton: false, timer: 1500 });
      });
    }

    function openPltRiwayatModal(kegiatanID) {
      var extObj = dbPltExtensions[kegiatanID];
      var contentEl = document.getElementById('pltRiwayatContent');
      if (!contentEl) return;

      if (!extObj || !extObj.history || extObj.history.length === 0) {
        contentEl.innerHTML = '<p class="text-muted text-center py-3">Belum ada riwayat perpanjangan.</p>';
      } else {
        var html = '<div class="table-responsive"><table class="table table-sm table-bordered align-middle small mb-0"><thead class="table-light"><tr><th>Ke-</th><th>Nomor SK Perpanjangan</th><th>TMT Mulai</th><th>Batas Berakhir (+3 Bln)</th><th>Keterangan</th></tr></thead><tbody>';
        extObj.history.forEach(function(h, idx) {
          html += '<tr>' +
            '<td class="text-center fw-bold">' + (idx + 1) + '</td>' +
            '<td>' + esc(h.noSK) + '</td>' +
            '<td>' + safeDate(h.tmtMulai) + '</td>' +
            '<td class="text-danger fw-semibold">' + safeDate(h.tmtBerakhir) + '</td>' +
            '<td>' + esc(h.keterangan || '-') + '</td>' +
          '</tr>';
        });
        html += '</tbody></table></div>';
        contentEl.innerHTML = html;
      }

      new bootstrap.Modal(document.getElementById('pltRiwayatModal')).show();
    }

    var PDFJS_BASE = 'https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/pdf.js/3.11.174/';

    function dataUrlToBytes(dataUrl) {
      var bin = atob(dataUrl.split(',')[1]);
      var arr = new Uint8Array(bin.length);
      for (var i = 0; i < bin.length; i++) arr[i] = bin.charCodeAt(i);
      return arr;
    }

    function dataUrlToBlobUrl(dataUrl) {
      var mime = dataUrl.split(',')[0].match(/:(.*?);/)[1];
      return URL.createObjectURL(new Blob([dataUrlToBytes(dataUrl)], { type: mime }));
    }

    // PDF tidak bisa ditampilkan lewat <iframe> di dalam aplikasi Apps Script
    // (frame ber-sandbox), jadi dirender ke <canvas> dengan pdf.js.
    function loadPdfJs() {
      if (window.pdfjsLib) return Promise.resolve(window.pdfjsLib);
      return new Promise(function(resolve, reject) {
        var s = document.createElement('script');
        s.src = PDFJS_BASE + 'pdf.min.js';
        s.onload = function() {
          if (!window.pdfjsLib) return reject(new Error('pdf.js tidak tersedia'));
          window.pdfjsLib.GlobalWorkerOptions.workerSrc = PDFJS_BASE + 'pdf.worker.min.js';
          resolve(window.pdfjsLib);
        };
        s.onerror = function() { reject(new Error('Gagal memuat pustaka penampil PDF')); };
        document.head.appendChild(s);
      });
    }

    function renderPdfInto(container, dataUrl) {
      return loadPdfJs().then(function(pdfjsLib) {
        return pdfjsLib.getDocument({ data: dataUrlToBytes(dataUrl) }).promise;
      }).then(function(pdf) {
        container.innerHTML = '';
        var total = Math.min(pdf.numPages, 15);
        var chain = Promise.resolve();
        for (var n = 1; n <= total; n++) {
          (function(pageNo) {
            chain = chain.then(function() {
              return pdf.getPage(pageNo).then(function(page) {
                var viewport = page.getViewport({ scale: 1.25 });
                var canvas = document.createElement('canvas');
                canvas.width = viewport.width;
                canvas.height = viewport.height;
                canvas.style.cssText = 'width:100%;height:auto;display:block;margin:0 auto 8px;background:#fff;box-shadow:0 1px 4px rgba(0,0,0,.2);';
                container.appendChild(canvas);
                return page.render({ canvasContext: canvas.getContext('2d'), viewport: viewport }).promise;
              });
            });
          })(n);
        }
        return chain.then(function() {
          if (pdf.numPages > total) {
            var note = document.createElement('div');
            note.className = 'small text-muted text-center py-2';
            note.textContent = 'Menampilkan ' + total + ' dari ' + pdf.numPages + ' halaman. Unduh berkas untuk melihat semuanya.';
            container.appendChild(note);
          }
        });
      });
    }

    function showLampiran(dataUrl, keg, fileName) {
      var isImage = dataUrl.indexOf('data:image') === 0;
      var name = fileName || ('Lampiran_' + keg.kegiatanID + (isImage ? '.jpg' : '.pdf'));
      var blobUrl = dataUrlToBlobUrl(dataUrl);

      var toolbar =
        '<div class="d-flex justify-content-between align-items-center mb-2 flex-wrap gap-2">' +
          '<small class="text-muted text-start">' + esc(name) + (keg.nomorSK ? ' &middot; SK: ' + esc(keg.nomorSK) : '') + '</small>' +
          '<span>' +
            '<a class="btn btn-sm btn-outline-secondary me-1" href="' + blobUrl + '" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><i class="fa-solid fa-up-right-from-square me-1"></i>Buka di tab baru</a>' +
            '<a class="btn btn-sm btn-primary" href="' + blobUrl + '" download="' + esc(name) + '"><i class="fa-solid fa-download me-1"></i>Unduh</a>' +
          '</span>' +
        '</div>';

      var body = isImage
        ? '<div style="max-height:70vh;overflow:auto;background:#f1f5f9;padding:8px;"><img src="' + dataUrl + '" alt="Lampiran" style="max-width:100%;height:auto;display:block;margin:0 auto;"></div>'
        : '<div id="lampiranViewer" style="max-height:70vh;overflow:auto;background:#f1f5f9;padding:8px;"><div class="text-center text-muted py-5"><span class="spinner-border spinner-border-sm me-2"></span>Memuat dokumen...</div></div>';

      Swal.fire({
        title: 'Berkas SK / Surat',
        html: toolbar + body,
        width: '85%',
        showCloseButton: true,
        showConfirmButton: false,
        didOpen: function() {
          if (isImage) return;
          var box = document.getElementById('lampiranViewer');
          renderPdfInto(box, dataUrl).catch(function(err) {
            box.innerHTML = '<div class="text-center py-5"><i class="fa-solid fa-triangle-exclamation text-warning fa-2x mb-2"></i>' +
              '<p class="mb-1">Dokumen tidak dapat ditampilkan di sini.</p>' +
              '<p class="small text-muted mb-0">' + esc(err && err.message ? err.message : '') + ' Gunakan tombol <b>Unduh</b> untuk membuka berkasnya.</p></div>';
          });
        }
      });
    }


    // ===== RIWAYAT PERUBAHAN DATA KEGIATAN =====
    var dbRiwayat = [], riwayatLoaded = false;
    function refreshRiwayat(force) {
      if (typeof google === 'undefined') { riwayatLoaded = true; renderRiwayatView(); return Promise.resolve(); }
      if (riwayatLoaded && !force) { /* tetap segarkan di latar belakang */ }
      var c = document.getElementById('riwayatContainer');
      if (c && !riwayatLoaded) c.innerHTML = '<div class="text-center text-muted py-4"><span class="spinner-border spinner-border-sm me-2"></span>Memuat riwayat...</div>';
      return gasRun('getRiwayatPerubahan').then(function(list) {
        dbRiwayat = Array.isArray(list) ? list : []; riwayatLoaded = true; renderRiwayatView();
      }).catch(function(err) { if (c) c.innerHTML = '<div class="text-center text-danger py-4">' + esc(err.message || 'Gagal memuat riwayat') + '</div>'; });
    }
    function renderRiwayatView() {
      var c = document.getElementById('riwayatContainer');
      if (!c) return;
      var q = ((document.getElementById('searchRiwayat') || {}).value || '').toLowerCase();
      var aksi = (document.getElementById('filterRiwayatAksi') || {}).value || '';
      var list = dbRiwayat.filter(function(r) {
        if (aksi && r.aksi !== aksi) return false;
        return !q || [r.nama, r.nip, r.username, r.namaJenisKegiatan].some(function(v) { return v && String(v).toLowerCase().indexOf(q) !== -1; });
      });
      if (!list.length) { c.innerHTML = '<div class="text-center text-muted py-5"><i class="fa-solid fa-clock-rotate-left fa-2x mb-2 opacity-50 d-block"></i>Belum ada riwayat perubahan.</div>'; return; }
      var h = '<div class="table-responsive"><table class="table table-custom table-hover align-top mb-0"><thead><tr><th style="width:140px">Waktu</th><th style="width:80px">Aksi</th><th>Pegawai</th><th>Jenis Kegiatan</th><th style="width:130px">Oleh</th><th>Rincian Perubahan</th><th style="width:60px"></th></tr></thead><tbody>';
      list.slice(0, 300).forEach(function(r) {
        var del = r.aksi === 'HAPUS';
        var det = (r.perubahan || []).map(function(x) {
          return del
            ? '<div class="small"><span class="text-muted">' + esc(x.f) + ':</span> ' + esc(x.a) + '</div>'
            : '<div class="small"><span class="text-muted">' + esc(x.f) + ':</span> <span class="text-danger text-decoration-line-through">' + esc(x.a || '-') + '</span> &rarr; <strong class="text-success">' + esc(x.b || '-') + '</strong></div>';
        }).join('') || '<small class="text-muted">Tidak ada perubahan isian.</small>';
        if (del) det = '<details><summary class="small text-danger fw-semibold" style="cursor:pointer">Data yang dihapus</summary>' + det + '</details>';
        h += '<tr><td><small>' + esc(r.waktu || '-') + '</small></td>' +
          '<td><span class="badge ' + (del ? 'bg-danger' : 'bg-primary') + '">' + (del ? 'HAPUS' : 'EDIT') + '</span></td>' +
          '<td><strong class="d-block small text-dark">' + esc(r.nama || '-') + '</strong><small class="text-muted">' + esc(r.nip || '-') + '</small></td>' +
          '<td><small>' + esc(r.namaJenisKegiatan || '-') + '</small></td>' +
          '<td><small>' + esc(r.username || '-') + '</small></td>' +
          '<td>' + det + '</td>' +
          '<td class="text-end text-nowrap">' + (r.bisaPulihkan ? '<button class="btn btn-sm btn-success me-1" title="Kembalikan data kegiatan ini beserta pangkat/jabatan/unor pegawai" onclick="pulihkanKegiatanAction(\'' + escJs(r.logID) + '\')"><i class="fa-solid fa-rotate-left me-1"></i>Pulihkan</button>' : (r.dipulihkan ? '<span class="badge bg-success-subtle text-success border me-1" title="' + esc(r.dipulihkan) + '">Dipulihkan</span>' : '')) + '<button class="btn btn-sm btn-outline-danger" title="Hapus catatan ini" onclick="hapusRiwayatAction(\'' + escJs(r.logID) + '\')"><i class="fa-solid fa-trash-can"></i></button></td></tr>';
      });
      c.innerHTML = h + '</tbody></table></div>' + (list.length > 300 ? '<div class="text-center small text-muted py-2">Menampilkan 300 dari ' + list.length + ' catatan. Gunakan pencarian.</div>' : '');
    }

    // ===== BACKUP DATA KEGIATAN =====
    function backupDataKegiatan() {
      var isAdmin = currentUser && currentUser.role === 'Admin';
      Swal.fire({
        title: 'Backup Data Kegiatan',
        html: 'Pilih cara backup seluruh data kegiatan (' + dbKegiatan.length + ' data).',
        icon: 'question', showDenyButton: !!isAdmin, showCancelButton: true,
        confirmButtonText: '<i class="fa-solid fa-file-excel me-1"></i> Unduh Excel',
        denyButtonText: '<i class="fa-brands fa-google-drive me-1"></i> Simpan ke Drive',
        cancelButtonText: 'Batal'
      }).then(function(r) {
        if (r.isConfirmed) { exportDataKegiatanToExcel(false); return; }
        if (!r.isDenied) return;
        if (typeof google === 'undefined') return Swal.fire('Simulasi', 'Backup ke Drive hanya berjalan di server Apps Script.', 'info');
        Swal.fire({ title: 'Membuat backup...', allowOutsideClick: false, didOpen: function() { Swal.showLoading(); } });
        gasRun('backupDataKegiatan').then(function(res) {
          Swal.fire({ icon: res.success ? 'success' : 'error', title: res.success ? 'Backup Berhasil' : 'Gagal', html: esc(res.message || '') + (res.url ? '<br><a href="' + esc(res.url) + '" target="_blank" rel="noopener">Buka file backup</a><br><small class="text-muted">Tersimpan di folder SIAMP_Backup pada Google Drive.</small>' : '') });
        }).catch(function(e) { Swal.fire('Error', e.message || 'Gagal membuat backup', 'error'); });
      });
    }

    // ===== PULIHKAN DATA KEGIATAN YANG DIHAPUS =====
    function pulihkanKegiatanAction(logID) {
      Swal.fire({
        title: 'Pulihkan data kegiatan ini?',
        html: 'Data kegiatan dikembalikan seperti sebelum dihapus, dan pangkat/jabatan/unor pegawai diperbarui kembali sesuai kegiatan tersebut (bila TMT-nya masih yang terbaru).',
        icon: 'question', showCancelButton: true, confirmButtonText: 'Ya, pulihkan', cancelButtonText: 'Batal'
      }).then(function(r) {
        if (!r.isConfirmed) return;
        if (typeof google === 'undefined') return Swal.fire('Simulasi', 'Pemulihan hanya berjalan di server Apps Script.', 'info');
        Swal.fire({ title: 'Memulihkan...', allowOutsideClick: false, didOpen: function() { Swal.showLoading(); } });
        gasRun('pulihkanKegiatan', logID).then(function(res) {
          if (res && res.success) {
            Swal.fire({ icon: 'success', title: 'Berhasil', text: res.message, timer: 1600, showConfirmButton: false });
            fetchKegiatanData(true); fetchPegawaiData(true); refreshRiwayat(true);
          } else Swal.fire('Gagal', (res && res.message) || 'Tidak dapat memulihkan', 'error');
        }).catch(function(e) { Swal.fire('Error', e.message || 'Gagal memulihkan', 'error'); });
      });
    }

    // ===== HAPUS CATATAN RIWAYAT PERUBAHAN =====
    function hapusRiwayatAction(logID) {
      var all = logID === 'ALL';
      Swal.fire({
        title: all ? 'Hapus semua catatan riwayat?' : 'Hapus catatan ini?',
        text: 'Catatan riwayat yang dihapus tidak dapat dikembalikan. Data kegiatan tidak terpengaruh.',
        icon: 'warning', showCancelButton: true, confirmButtonColor: '#dc3545', confirmButtonText: 'Ya, hapus', cancelButtonText: 'Batal'
      }).then(function(r) {
        if (!r.isConfirmed) return;
        var done = function() {
          dbRiwayat = all ? [] : dbRiwayat.filter(function(x) { return x.logID !== logID; });
          renderRiwayatView();
        };
        if (typeof google === 'undefined') return done();
        gasRun('hapusRiwayatPerubahan', logID).then(function(res) {
          if (res && res.success) done(); else Swal.fire('Gagal', (res && res.message) || 'Tidak dapat menghapus', 'error');
        }).catch(function(e) { Swal.fire('Error', e.message || 'Gagal menghapus', 'error'); });
      });
    }

    // ===== PERCEPATAN LIHAT BERKAS =====
    var lampiranCache = {}, lampiranPending = {};
    function fetchLampiran(keg) {
      var key = keg.kegiatanID + '|' + keg.fileData;
      if (lampiranCache[key]) return Promise.resolve(lampiranCache[key]);
      if (lampiranPending[key]) return lampiranPending[key];
      lampiranPending[key] = gasRun('getLampiran', keg.kegiatanID, keg.fileData).then(function(res) {
        delete lampiranPending[key];
        if (res && res.success) lampiranCache[key] = res;
        return res;
      }).catch(function(e) { delete lampiranPending[key]; throw e; });
      return lampiranPending[key];
    }
    document.addEventListener('mouseover', function(e) {
      var b = e.target && e.target.closest ? e.target.closest('button[onclick^="previewUploadedDoc"]') : null;
      if (!b || b.__pf) return;
      b.__pf = true;
      var m = /previewUploadedDoc\('([^']+)'/.exec(b.getAttribute('onclick') || '');
      var keg = m && dbKegiatan.find(function(x) { return x.kegiatanID === m[1]; });
      if (keg && String(keg.fileData).indexOf('drive:') === 0 && typeof google !== 'undefined') fetchLampiran(keg).catch(function() {});
    });
    setTimeout(function() { try { if (typeof google !== 'undefined') loadPdfJs().catch(function() {}); } catch (e) {} }, 6000);

    // prefetch berkas yang tampil di layar (berurutan, maks 8) agar tombol "Lihat Berkas" langsung terbuka
    var prefetchTimer = null;
    function prefetchVisibleLampiran(containerId) {
      if (typeof google === 'undefined') return;
      clearTimeout(prefetchTimer);
      prefetchTimer = setTimeout(function() {
        var c = document.getElementById(containerId); if (!c) return;
        var ids = [];
        c.querySelectorAll('button[onclick^="previewUploadedDoc"]').forEach(function(b) {
          var m = /previewUploadedDoc\('([^']+)'/.exec(b.getAttribute('onclick') || '');
          if (m && ids.length < 8) ids.push(m[1]);
        });
        var run = function() {
          var id = ids.shift(); if (!id) return;
          var keg = dbKegiatan.find(function(x) { return x.kegiatanID === id; });
          if (keg && String(keg.fileData).indexOf('drive:') === 0) fetchLampiran(keg).then(run, run); else run();
        };
        run();
      }, 1200);
    }

    function previewUploadedDoc(kegiatanID) {
      var keg = dbKegiatan.find(function(x){ return x.kegiatanID === kegiatanID; });
      if (!keg || !keg.fileData) return Swal.fire('Informasi', 'Tidak ada berkas fisik yang terlampir pada kegiatan ini.', 'info');

      if (String(keg.fileData).indexOf('drive:') === 0) {
        Swal.fire({ title: 'Memuat berkas...', allowOutsideClick: false, didOpen: function() { Swal.showLoading(); } });
        fetchLampiran(keg).then(function(res) {
          Swal.close();
          if (!res || !res.success) return Swal.fire('Informasi', (res && res.message) || 'Berkas tidak ditemukan.', 'info');
          showLampiran(res.dataUrl, keg, res.name);
        }).catch(function(err) { Swal.close(); Swal.fire('Error', err.message, 'error'); });
      } else {
        showLampiran(keg.fileData, keg);
      }
    }


    function renderSelectPegawaiList() {
      var tbody = document.getElementById('selectPegawaiTableBody');
      if (!tbody) return;

      if (!isPegawaiLoaded) {
        tbody.innerHTML = '<tr><td colspan="5" class="text-center text-muted py-4"><span class="spinner-border spinner-border-sm me-2"></span>Memuat data pegawai...</td></tr>';
        return;
      }
      var search = (document.getElementById('selectPegawaiSearch') ? document.getElementById('selectPegawaiSearch').value : '').trim().toLowerCase();
      if (search.length < 2) {
        tbody.innerHTML = '<tr><td colspan="5" class="text-center text-muted py-4">Ketik minimal 2 huruf (nama, NIP/NIPPPK, jabatan) untuk mencari pegawai.</td></tr>';
        return;
      }

      var filtered = [], total = 0;
      for (var i = 0; i < dbPegawai.length; i++) {
        if (pegKey(dbPegawai[i]).indexOf(search) !== -1) { total++; if (filtered.length < 50) filtered.push(dbPegawai[i]); }
      }

      if (filtered.length === 0) {
        tbody.innerHTML = '<tr><td colspan="5" class="text-center text-muted py-4">Tidak ada pegawai ditemukan.</td></tr>';
        return;
      }

      var html = '';
      filtered.forEach(function(p) {
        var fullName = pegFullName(p);
        var nipCode = isNipStatus(p.statusPegawai) ? (p.nip || '-') : (p.nipppk || '-');
        var badge = isNipStatus(p.statusPegawai) ? 'bg-info-subtle text-info' : (p.statusPegawai === 'PPPK' ? 'bg-success-subtle text-success' : 'bg-warning-subtle text-warning');

        html += '<tr>' +
          '<td><strong class="text-dark d-block">' + esc(fullName) + '</strong><small class="text-muted">NIP/NIPPPK: ' + esc(nipCode) + '</small></td>' +
          '<td><span class="badge ' + badge + ' border">' + esc(p.statusPegawai) + '</span></td>' +
          '<td><small class="text-dark">' + esc(p.jabatan || '-') + '</small></td>' +
          '<td><small class="text-muted">' + esc(p.unitInduk || '-') + '</small></td>' +
          '<td class="text-end">' +
            '<button type="button" class="btn btn-sm btn-primary px-3" onclick="selectPegawaiForKegiatan(\'' + escJs(p.pegawaiID) + '\')">' +
              '<i class="fa-solid fa-check me-1"></i> Pilih' +
            '</button>' +
          '</td>' +
        '</tr>';
      });
      if (total > filtered.length) html += '<tr><td colspan="5" class="text-center small text-muted py-2">Menampilkan 50 dari ' + total + ' hasil. Persempit pencarian.</td></tr>';

      tbody.innerHTML = html;
    }

    var pegEnsurePromise = null;
    function ensurePegawaiLoaded() {
      if (isPegawaiLoaded) return Promise.resolve();
      if (pegEnsurePromise) return pegEnsurePromise;
      var base = bootstrapPromise
        ? bootstrapPromise.then(function() { if (!isPegawaiLoaded) return fetchPegawaiData(true); })
        : fetchPegawaiData(true);
      pegEnsurePromise = base.then(function() { pegEnsurePromise = null; warmPegKeys(); }, function() { pegEnsurePromise = null; });
      return pegEnsurePromise;
    }
    // siapkan indeks pencarian di latar belakang (bertahap) agar pencarian pertama langsung cepat
    function warmPegKeys() {
      var i = 0, arr = dbPegawai;
      (function step() {
        var end = Math.min(i + 500, arr.length);
        for (; i < end; i++) pegKey(arr[i]);
        if (i < arr.length) setTimeout(step, 0);
      })();
    }

    function openSelectPegawaiModal(context) {
      selectPegawaiTargetContext = context || 'page_input';
      document.getElementById('selectPegawaiSearch').value = '';
      renderSelectPegawaiList();
      var modalEl = document.getElementById('selectPegawaiModal');
      (bootstrap.Modal.getOrCreateInstance(modalEl)).show();
      setTimeout(function() { var si = document.getElementById('selectPegawaiSearch'); if (si) si.focus(); }, 300);
      if (!isPegawaiLoaded) ensurePegawaiLoaded().then(function() { renderSelectPegawaiList(); });
    }

    function renderSelectPegawaiList() {
      var tbody = document.getElementById('selectPegawaiTableBody');
      if (!tbody) return;

      var search = (document.getElementById('selectPegawaiSearch')?.value || '').toLowerCase();

      var filtered = dbPegawai.filter(function(x) {
        return search === '' ||
          (x.nama && x.nama.toLowerCase().includes(search)) ||
          (x.nip && x.nip.toLowerCase().includes(search)) ||
          (x.nipppk && x.nipppk.toLowerCase().includes(search)) ||
          (x.jabatan && x.jabatan.toLowerCase().includes(search)) ||
          (x.unitInduk && x.unitInduk.toLowerCase().includes(search));
      });

      if (filtered.length === 0) {
        tbody.innerHTML = '<tr><td colspan="5" class="text-center text-muted py-4">Tidak ada pegawai ditemukan.</td></tr>';
        return;
      }

      var html = '';
      filtered.forEach(function(p) {
        var fullName = (p.gelarDepan ? p.gelarDepan + ' ' : '') + p.nama + (p.gelarBelakang ? ', ' + p.gelarBelakang : '');
        var nipCode = isNipStatus(p.statusPegawai) ? (p.nip || '-') : (p.nipppk || '-');
        var badge = isNipStatus(p.statusPegawai) ? 'bg-info-subtle text-info' : (p.statusPegawai === 'PPPK' ? 'bg-success-subtle text-success' : 'bg-warning-subtle text-warning');

        html += '<tr>' +
          '<td><strong class="text-dark d-block">' + esc(fullName) + '</strong><small class="text-muted">NIP/NIPPPK: ' + esc(nipCode) + '</small></td>' +
          '<td><span class="badge ' + badge + ' border">' + esc(p.statusPegawai) + '</span></td>' +
          '<td><small class="text-dark">' + esc(p.jabatan || '-') + '</small></td>' +
          '<td><small class="text-muted">' + esc(p.unitInduk || '-') + '</small></td>' +
          '<td class="text-end">' +
            '<button type="button" class="btn btn-sm btn-primary px-3" onclick="selectPegawaiForKegiatan(\'' + escJs(p.pegawaiID) + '\')">' +
              '<i class="fa-solid fa-check me-1"></i> Pilih' +
            '</button>' +
          '</td>' +
        '</tr>';
      });

      tbody.innerHTML = html;
    }

    function exportPegawaiToExcel() {
      if (dbPegawai.length === 0) return Swal.fire('Informasi', 'Belum ada data pegawai untuk diekspor.', 'info');

      var exportData = dbPegawai.map(function(p, i) {
        return {
          'No': i + 1,
          'ID Pegawai': p.pegawaiID,
          'Status Pegawai': p.statusPegawai,
          'Gelar Depan': p.gelarDepan || '',
          'Nama': p.nama || '',
          'Gelar Belakang': p.gelarBelakang || '',
          'NIP (PNS)': p.nip || '',
          'NIPPPK (PPPK/PW)': p.nipppk || '',
          'Pangkat': p.pangkat || '',
          'Golongan': p.golongan || '',
          'Jabatan': p.jabatan || '',
          'Pendidikan': p.pendidikan || '',
          'Alamat Rumah': p.alamatRumah || '',
          'UNOR': p.unor || '',
          'Unit Induk': p.unitInduk || ''
        };
      });

      var ws = XLSX.utils.json_to_sheet(exportData);
      var wb = XLSX.utils.book_new();
      XLSX.utils.book_append_sheet(wb, ws, "Database Pegawai");

      XLSX.writeFile(wb, "Data_Pegawai_SIAMP_Pasuruan_" + new Date().toISOString().substring(0, 10) + ".xlsx");
    }

          'GelarBelakang': 'M.Si',
          'NIP': '197504121996031002',

          'Pangkat': 'Pembina Utama Muda (IV/c)',

          'Jabatan': 'Kepala Dinas',
          'Pendidikan': 'S2 Administrasi Publik',
          'AlamatRumah': 'Jl. Hayam Wuruk No. 12',
          'UNOR': 'Sekretariat Dinas',
          'UnitInduk': 'Dinas Kesehatan'
        }

      var ws = XLSX.utils.json_to_sheet(templateData);
      var wb = XLSX.utils.book_new();

    function openImportPegawaiModal() {
      document.getElementById('importPegawaiFile').value = '';
      document.getElementById('importPreviewContainer').classList.add('d-none');
      document.getElementById('btnSubmitImport').disabled = true;
      parsedImportPegawai = [];
      new bootstrap.Modal(document.getElementById('importPegawaiModal')).show();
    }

    function handleImportPegawaiFile(e) {
      var files = e.target.files;
      if (!files || files.length === 0) return;
      var file = files[0];

      var reader = new FileReader();
      reader.onload = function(evt) {
        try {
          var data = evt.target.result;
          var workbook = XLSX.read(data, { type: 'binary' });
          var firstSheet = workbook.SheetNames[0];
          var rawRows = XLSX.utils.sheet_to_json(workbook.Sheets[firstSheet]);

          if (!rawRows || rawRows.length === 0) {
            Swal.fire('Error', 'File Excel kosong atau tidak terbaca.', 'error');
            return;
          }

          parsedImportPegawai = rawRows.map(function(r, idx) {
            var getVal = function(keys) {
              for (var i = 0; i < keys.length; i++) {
                if (r[keys[i]] !== undefined) return String(r[keys[i]]).trim();
              }
              return '';
            };

            var st = getVal(['StatusPegawai', 'Status Pegawai', 'STATUS', 'Status']).toUpperCase();
            if (st !== 'PNS' && st !== 'CPNS' && st !== 'PPPK' && st !== 'PW') st = 'PNS';

            return {
              pegawaiID: 'PEG-IMP-' + (Date.now() + idx),
              statusPegawai: st,
              nama: getVal(['Nama', 'NAMA', 'Nama Lengkap']),
              gelarDepan: getVal(['GelarDepan', 'Gelar Depan', 'GELAR DEPAN']),
              gelarBelakang: getVal(['GelarBelakang', 'Gelar Belakang', 'GELAR BELAKANG']),
              nip: getVal(['NIP', 'Nip', 'NIP (PNS)']),
              nipppk: getVal(['NIPPPK', 'Nipppk', 'NIPPPK (PPPK/PW)']),
              pangkat: getVal(['Pangkat', 'PANGKAT']),
              golongan: getVal(['Golongan', 'GOLONGAN']),
              jabatan: getVal(['Jabatan', 'JABATAN']),
              pendidikan: getVal(['Pendidikan', 'PENDIDIKAN']),
              alamatRumah: getVal(['AlamatRumah', 'Alamat Rumah', 'ALAMAT RUMAH', 'Alamat']),
              unor: getVal(['UNOR', 'Unor', 'Unit Organisasi']),
              unitInduk: getVal(['UnitInduk', 'Unit Induk', 'UNIT INDUK', 'Perangkat Daerah'])
            };
          }).filter(function(x) { return x.nama !== ''; });

          if (parsedImportPegawai.length === 0) {
            Swal.fire('Error', 'Tidak ada data pegawai valid dengan kolom Nama.', 'warning');
            return;
          }

          renderImportPreviewTable();
        } catch (err) {
          Swal.fire('Error Reading Excel', err.message || 'Format file tidak valid', 'error');
        }
      };
      reader.readAsBinaryString(file);
    }

    function renderImportPreviewTable() {
      var container = document.getElementById('importPreviewContainer');
      var tbody = document.getElementById('importPreviewTableBody');
      var countEl = document.getElementById('importTotalCount');
      var btnSubmit = document.getElementById('btnSubmitImport');

      countEl.innerText = parsedImportPegawai.length;
      container.classList.remove('d-none');
      btnSubmit.disabled = parsedImportPegawai.length === 0;

      var html = '';
      parsedImportPegawai.slice(0, 15).forEach(function(item) {
        var fullName = (item.gelarDepan ? item.gelarDepan + ' ' : '') + item.nama + (item.gelarBelakang ? ', ' + item.gelarBelakang : '');
        var nipCode = isNipStatus(item.statusPegawai) ? (item.nip || '-') : (item.nipppk || '-');
        html += '<tr>' +
          '<td><span class="badge bg-light text-dark border">' + esc(item.statusPegawai) + '</span></td>' +
          '<td><strong class="text-dark">' + esc(fullName) + '</strong></td>' +
          '<td><small class="text-muted">' + esc(nipCode) + '</small></td>' +
          '<td><small class="text-dark">' + esc(item.jabatan || '-') + '</small></td>' +
          '<td><small class="text-muted">' + esc(item.unitInduk || '-') + '</small></td>' +
        '</tr>';
      });

      if (parsedImportPegawai.length > 15) {
        html += '<tr><td colspan="5" class="text-center text-muted small py-2">... dan ' + (parsedImportPegawai.length - 15) + ' data pegawai lainnya.</td></tr>';
      }

      tbody.innerHTML = html;
    }

    function submitImportPegawai() {
      if (parsedImportPegawai.length === 0) return;

      var modalEl = document.getElementById('importPegawaiModal');
      var modalInst = bootstrap.Modal.getInstance(modalEl);
      if (modalInst) modalInst.hide();

      var list = parsedImportPegawai.slice();
      Swal.fire({ title: 'Mengimpor ' + list.length + ' pegawai...', text: 'Data lama dipertahankan; NIP/NIPPPK yang sama hanya diperbarui.', allowOutsideClick: false, didOpen: function() { Swal.showLoading(); } });

      if (typeof google !== 'undefined') {
        gasRun('importPegawaiBatch', list)
          .then(function(res) {
            Swal.close();
            if (res.success) { fetchPegawaiData(true); Swal.fire('Impor Selesai', res.message, 'success'); }
            else Swal.fire('Error', res.message, 'error');
          })
          .catch(function(err) { Swal.close(); Swal.fire('Error', err.message || 'Gagal mengimpor', 'error'); });
      } else {
        setTimeout(function() {
          var digits = function(v) { return String(v || '').replace(/\D/g, ''); };
          var upd = 0, add = 0;
          list.forEach(function(p) {
            var k = digits(p.nip) || digits(p.nipppk);
            var hit = k ? dbPegawai.find(function(x) { return digits(x.nip) === k || digits(x.nipppk) === k; }) : null;
            if (hit) { Object.keys(p).forEach(function(f) { if (p[f] !== '' && p[f] != null) hit[f] = p[f]; }); upd++; }
            else { dbPegawai.unshift(Object.assign({}, p, { pegawaiID: 'PEG-IMP-' + Date.now() + '-' + add })); add++; }
          });
          renderPegawaiTable(); populateUnitIndukDropdowns(); Swal.close();
          Swal.fire('Impor Selesai (Simulasi)', add + ' pegawai baru ditambahkan, ' + upd + ' data lama diperbarui (tidak ada yang dihapus).', 'success');
        }, 700);
      }
    }

    function togglePegawaiFieldStatus() {
      var st = document.getElementById('modal_peg_status').value;
      var lblNip = document.getElementById('label_nip_nipppk');
      var wrapPangkat = document.getElementById('wrapper_pangkat');
      var wrapGolongan = document.getElementById('wrapper_golongan');

      if (isNipStatus(st)) {
        lblNip.innerHTML = 'NIP (' + st + ') <span class="text-danger">*</span>';
        wrapPangkat.style.display = '';
        wrapGolongan.style.display = 'none';
      } else if (st === 'PPPK') {
        lblNip.innerHTML = 'NIPPPK (PPPK) <span class="text-danger">*</span>';
        wrapPangkat.style.display = 'none';
        wrapGolongan.style.display = '';
      } else {
        lblNip.innerHTML = 'NIPPPK / ID (PW) <span class="text-danger">*</span>';
        wrapPangkat.style.display = 'none';
        wrapGolongan.style.display = 'none';
      }
    }

    function openPegawaiModal(pegawaiID) {
      document.getElementById('pegawaiForm').reset();
      document.getElementById('modal_pegawai_id').value = '';

      var modalTitle = document.getElementById('modalPegawaiTitle');

      if (pegawaiID) {
        var item = dbPegawai.find(function(x){ return x.pegawaiID === pegawaiID; });
        if (!item) return;
        modalTitle.innerHTML = '<i class="fa-solid fa-user-pen text-primary me-2"></i>Edit Data Pegawai';
        document.getElementById('modal_pegawai_id').value = item.pegawaiID;
        document.getElementById('modal_peg_status').value = item.statusPegawai || 'PNS';
        document.getElementById('modal_peg_nama').value = item.nama || '';
        document.getElementById('modal_peg_gelardepan').value = item.gelarDepan || '';
        document.getElementById('modal_peg_gelarbelakang').value = item.gelarBelakang || '';
        document.getElementById('modal_peg_nip').value = isNipStatus(item.statusPegawai) ? item.nip : item.nipppk;
        document.getElementById('modal_peg_pangkat').value = item.pangkat || '';
        document.getElementById('modal_peg_golongan').value = item.golongan || '';
        document.getElementById('modal_peg_jabatan').value = item.jabatan || '';
        document.getElementById('modal_peg_pendidikan').value = item.pendidikan || '';
        document.getElementById('modal_peg_alamat').value = item.alamatRumah || '';
        document.getElementById('modal_peg_unor').value = item.unor || '';
        document.getElementById('modal_peg_unitinduk').value = item.unitInduk || '';
      } else {
        modalTitle.innerHTML = '<i class="fa-solid fa-user-plus text-primary me-2"></i>Tambah Master Data Pegawai';
        document.getElementById('modal_peg_status').value = 'PNS';
      }

      togglePegawaiFieldStatus();
      new bootstrap.Modal(document.getElementById('pegawaiModal')).show();
    }

    function editPegawai(pegawaiID) {
      var p = dbPegawai.find(function(x){ return x.pegawaiID === pegawaiID; });
      requireIzin('EDIT_PEGAWAI', pegawaiID, p ? p.nama : pegawaiID).then(function(ok) { if (ok) openPegawaiModal(pegawaiID); });
    }

    function viewDetailPegawai(pegawaiID) {
      selectedProfilPegawaiID = pegawaiID;
      showPage('profil');
    }


    // ===== Pencarian pegawai cepat (indeks teks per objek, hasil dibatasi) =====
    var pegKeyMap = new WeakMap();
    function pegKey(p) {
      var k = pegKeyMap.get(p);
      if (k === undefined) {
        k = [p.nama, p.nip, p.nipppk, p.jabatan, p.unor, p.unitInduk, p.statusPegawai].join('\u0001').toLowerCase();
        pegKeyMap.set(p, k);
      }
      return k;
    }
    function pangkatGolText(p) {
      var pk = String(p.pangkat || '').trim(), gl = String(p.golongan || '').trim();
      if (isNipStatus(p.statusPegawai)) {
        if (pk && gl && pk.indexOf('(') === -1 && pk.toUpperCase().indexOf(gl.toUpperCase()) === -1) return pk + ' (' + gl + ')';
        return pk || gl;
      }
      return gl ? gl : pk;
    }
    function pegFullName(p) { return (p.gelarDepan ? p.gelarDepan + ' ' : '') + p.nama + (p.gelarBelakang ? ', ' + p.gelarBelakang : ''); }

    // ===== PILIH PEGAWAI DARI DATABASE (Form Input) - pencarian langsung =====
    function inputPegSearch() {
      var box = document.getElementById('inputPegResults');
      var inp = document.getElementById('inputPegSearch');
      if (!box || !inp) return;
      var q = inp.value.trim().toLowerCase();
      if (q.length < 2) { box.classList.add('d-none'); box.innerHTML = ''; return; }
      if (!isPegawaiLoaded) {
        box.innerHTML = '<div class="list-group-item text-muted small"><span class="spinner-border spinner-border-sm me-2"></span>Memuat data pegawai...</div>';
        box.classList.remove('d-none');
        ensurePegawaiLoaded().then(inputPegSearch);
        return;
      }
      var out = [], total = 0;
      for (var i = 0; i < dbPegawai.length; i++) {
        var p = dbPegawai[i];
        if (pegKey(p).indexOf(q) !== -1) { total++; if (out.length < 15) out.push(p); }
      }
      if (!out.length) { box.innerHTML = '<div class="list-group-item text-muted small">Tidak ada pegawai ditemukan.</div>'; box.classList.remove('d-none'); return; }
      var h = '';
      out.forEach(function(p) {
        var nipCode = isNipStatus(p.statusPegawai) ? p.nip : p.nipppk;
        h += '<button type="button" class="list-group-item list-group-item-action py-2" onclick="onSelectInputPegawai(\'' + escJs(p.pegawaiID) + '\')">' +
          '<strong class="d-block small text-dark">' + esc(pegFullName(p)) + '</strong>' +
          '<small class="text-muted">' + esc(p.statusPegawai) + ' &middot; ' + esc(nipCode || '-') + ' &middot; ' + esc(p.jabatan || '-') + '</small></button>';
      });
      if (total > out.length) h += '<div class="list-group-item small text-muted">Menampilkan 15 dari ' + total + ' hasil. Persempit pencarian.</div>';
      box.innerHTML = h;
      box.classList.remove('d-none');
    }
    function onSelectInputPegawai(pegID) {
      selectPegawaiTargetContext = 'page_input';
      selectPegawaiForKegiatan(pegID);
      var box = document.getElementById('inputPegResults');
      if (box) { box.classList.add('d-none'); box.innerHTML = ''; }
      var inp = document.getElementById('inputPegSearch');
      var p = dbPegawai.find(function(x) { return x.pegawaiID === pegID; });
      if (inp) inp.value = p ? pegFullName(p) : '';
    }
    document.addEventListener('click', function(e) {
      var box = document.getElementById('inputPegResults');
      if (box && !box.classList.contains('d-none') && e.target.id !== 'inputPegSearch' && !box.contains(e.target)) box.classList.add('d-none');
    });

    function profilSearch() {
      var box = document.getElementById('profilSearchResults');
      var inp = document.getElementById('profilSearchInput');
      if (!box || !inp) return;
      var q = inp.value.trim().toLowerCase();
      if (q.length < 2) { box.classList.add('d-none'); box.innerHTML = ''; return; }
      var out = [], total = 0;
      for (var i = 0; i < dbPegawai.length; i++) {
        var p = dbPegawai[i];
        if (pegKey(p).indexOf(q) !== -1) { total++; if (out.length < 15) out.push(p); }
      }
      if (!out.length) { box.innerHTML = '<div class="list-group-item text-muted small">Tidak ada pegawai ditemukan.</div>'; box.classList.remove('d-none'); return; }
      var h = '';
      out.forEach(function(p) {
        var nipCode = isNipStatus(p.statusPegawai) ? p.nip : p.nipppk;
        h += '<button type="button" class="list-group-item list-group-item-action py-2" onclick="onSelectProfilPegawai(\'' + escJs(p.pegawaiID) + '\')">' +
          '<strong class="d-block small text-dark">' + esc(pegFullName(p)) + '</strong>' +
          '<small class="text-muted">' + esc(p.statusPegawai) + ' &middot; ' + esc(nipCode || '-') + ' &middot; ' + esc(p.jabatan || '-') + '</small></button>';
      });
      if (total > out.length) h += '<div class="list-group-item small text-muted">Menampilkan 15 dari ' + total + ' hasil. Persempit pencarian.</div>';
      box.innerHTML = h;
      box.classList.remove('d-none');
    }

    function onSelectProfilPegawai(pegID) {
      selectedProfilPegawaiID = pegID;
      var box = document.getElementById('profilSearchResults');
      if (box) { box.classList.add('d-none'); box.innerHTML = ''; }
      renderProfilPegawaiView();
    }

    function renderProfilPegawaiView() {
      var container = document.getElementById('profilContainer');
      var inputEl = document.getElementById('profilSearchInput');
      if (!container) return;

      if (dbPegawai.length === 0) {
        container.innerHTML = '<div class="text-center py-5 bg-white rounded border"><i class="fa-solid fa-id-badge fa-3x text-muted mb-3 opacity-50"></i><p class="text-muted">Belum ada data pegawai terdaftar.</p></div>';
        return;
      }

      var targetPeg = dbPegawai.find(function(x){ return x.pegawaiID === selectedProfilPegawaiID; });
      if (!targetPeg) {
        container.innerHTML = '<div class="text-center py-5 bg-white rounded border"><i class="fa-solid fa-magnifying-glass fa-3x text-muted mb-3 opacity-50"></i><p class="fw-semibold text-dark mb-1">Cari pegawai untuk melihat profil</p><p class="text-muted small mb-0">Ketik nama atau NIP/NIPPPK pada kotak pencarian di atas.</p></div>';
        return;
      }
      if (inputEl && document.activeElement !== inputEl) inputEl.value = pegFullName(targetPeg);

      var fullName = (targetPeg.gelarDepan ? targetPeg.gelarDepan + ' ' : '') + targetPeg.nama + (targetPeg.gelarBelakang ? ', ' + targetPeg.gelarBelakang : '');
      var nipCode = isNipStatus(targetPeg.statusPegawai) ? (targetPeg.nip || '-') : (targetPeg.nipppk || '-');
      var nipLabel = isNipStatus(targetPeg.statusPegawai) ? 'NIP' : 'NIPPPK';
      var rankGol = pangkatGolText(targetPeg) || '-';

      var statusBadge = '';
      if (targetPeg.statusPegawai === 'CPNS') statusBadge = '<span class="badge bg-primary-subtle text-primary border px-3 py-1 fs-6">CPNS</span>';
      else if (targetPeg.statusPegawai === 'PNS') statusBadge = '<span class="badge bg-info-subtle text-info border px-3 py-1 fs-6">PNS</span>';
      else if (targetPeg.statusPegawai === 'PPPK') statusBadge = '<span class="badge bg-success-subtle text-success border px-3 py-1 fs-6">PPPK</span>';
      else statusBadge = '<span class="badge bg-warning-subtle text-warning border px-3 py-1 fs-6">PPPK Paruh Waktu</span>';

      var pegNip = targetPeg.nip || targetPeg.nipppk;
      var activities = dbKegiatan.filter(function(k) {
        return (k.pegawaiID && k.pegawaiID === targetPeg.pegawaiID) ||
               (pegNip && k.nip && String(k.nip).trim() === String(pegNip).trim());
      }).sort(function(a,b){
        return new Date(b.tanggalKegiatan || 0) - new Date(a.tanggalKegiatan || 0);
      });

      var html =
        '<div class="card card-custom border-0 shadow-sm p-4 mb-4 bg-white print-area">' +
          '<div class="d-flex align-items-center gap-4 pb-3 mb-4 border-bottom flex-wrap">' +
            '<div class="bg-primary text-white rounded-circle d-flex align-items-center justify-content-center shadow-sm" style="width:72px; height:72px; font-size:1.8rem; flex-shrink:0;">' +
              '<i class="fa-solid fa-user-tie"></i>' +
            '</div>' +
            '<div class="flex-grow-1">' +
              '<div class="d-flex align-items-center gap-2 mb-1 flex-wrap">' +
                '<h4 class="fw-bold text-dark mb-0">' + esc(fullName) + '</h4>' +
                statusBadge +
              '</div>' +
              '<p class="text-muted mb-0 small">' + nipLabel + ': <strong>' + esc(nipCode) + '</strong> | Jabatan: <strong>' + esc(targetPeg.jabatan || '-') + '</strong></p>' +
              '<span class="badge bg-light text-dark border mt-1">' + (targetPeg.unor ? esc(targetPeg.unor) + ' — ' : '') + (targetPeg.unitInduk || '-') + '</span>' +
            '</div>' +
            '<div class="no-print d-flex gap-2">' +
              '<button type="button" class="btn btn-outline-primary btn-sm px-3" onclick="editPegawai(\'' + escJs(targetPeg.pegawaiID) + '\')">' +
                '<i class="fa-solid fa-pen-to-square me-1"></i> Edit Pegawai' +
              '</button>' +
              '<button type="button" class="btn btn-primary btn-sm px-3" onclick="openSelectPegawaiModal(\'page_input\'); selectPegawaiForKegiatan(\'' + escJs(targetPeg.pegawaiID) + '\'); showPage(\'input\');">' +
                '<i class="fa-solid fa-plus me-1"></i> Input Kegiatan Baru' +
              '</button>' +
            '</div>' +
          '</div>' +

          '<div class="row g-3 mb-4">' +
            '<div class="col-md-6">' +
              '<div class="card border-0 bg-light p-3 rounded-3 h-100">' +
                '<h6 class="fw-bold text-primary mb-3"><i class="fa-solid fa-id-card me-2"></i>Identitas & Data Pribadi</h6>' +
                '<table class="table table-sm borderless mb-0 small text-dark">' +
                  '<tr><td class="text-muted" style="width:130px;">Nama Lengkap</td><td class="fw-semibold">: ' + esc(targetPeg.nama || '-') + '</td></tr>' +
                  '<tr><td class="text-muted">Gelar Depan</td><td>: ' + esc(targetPeg.gelarDepan || '-') + '</td></tr>' +
                  '<tr><td class="text-muted">Gelar Belakang</td><td>: ' + esc(targetPeg.gelarBelakang || '-') + '</td></tr>' +
                  '<tr><td class="text-muted">' + nipLabel + '</td><td class="fw-semibold">: ' + esc(nipCode) + '</td></tr>' +
                  '<tr><td class="text-muted">Status Pegawai</td><td>: ' + esc(targetPeg.statusPegawai) + '</td></tr>' +
                  '<tr><td class="text-muted">Pendidikan Terakhir</td><td>: ' + esc(targetPeg.pendidikan || '-') + '</td></tr>' +
                  '<tr><td class="text-muted">Alamat Rumah</td><td>: ' + esc(targetPeg.alamatRumah || '-') + '</td></tr>' +
                '</table>' +
              '</div>' +
            '</div>' +
            '<div class="col-md-6">' +
              '<div class="card border-0 bg-light p-3 rounded-3 h-100">' +
                '<h6 class="fw-bold text-primary mb-3"><i class="fa-solid fa-briefcase me-2"></i>Jabatan & Unit Kerja</h6>' +
                '<table class="table table-sm borderless mb-0 small text-dark">' +
                  '<tr><td class="text-muted" style="width:130px;">Jabatan Definitif</td><td class="fw-semibold">: ' + esc(targetPeg.jabatan || '-') + '</td></tr>' +
                  (targetPeg.jabatanPlt ? '<tr><td class="text-muted">Jabatan ' + esc(targetPeg.jenisPlt || 'PLT') + '</td><td class="fw-semibold text-warning-emphasis">: ' + esc(targetPeg.jabatanPlt) + (targetPeg.tmtPlt ? ' <small class="text-muted">(TMT ' + esc(safeDate(targetPeg.tmtPlt)) + ')</small>' : '') + '</td></tr>' : '') +
                  '<tr><td class="text-muted">Pangkat / Golongan</td><td>: ' + esc(rankGol) + '</td></tr>' +
                  '<tr><td class="text-muted">UNOR (Unit Organisasi)</td><td>: ' + esc(targetPeg.unor || '-') + '</td></tr>' +
                  '<tr><td class="text-muted">Unit Induk (PD)</td><td>: ' + esc(targetPeg.unitInduk || '-') + '</td></tr>' +
                  (targetPeg.jabatanPlt && targetPeg.unorPlt ? '<tr><td class="text-muted">UNOR ' + esc(targetPeg.jenisPlt || 'PLT') + '</td><td>: ' + esc(targetPeg.unorPlt) + '</td></tr>' : '') +
