                  '<tr><td class="text-muted">Data Lama</td><td>: <button type="button" class="btn btn-sm btn-outline-primary py-0 px-2" onclick="lihatRiwayatJabatan(\'' + escJs(targetPeg.pegawaiID) + '\')"><i class="fa-solid fa-clock-rotate-left me-1"></i>Lihat Data Lama Jabatan &amp; Unit Kerja</button></td></tr>' +
                  '<tr><td class="text-muted">Sumber Data</td><td>: ' + esc(targetPeg.sumberData || targetPeg.statusPegawai || '-') + '</td></tr>' +
                  '<tr><td class="text-muted">Total Kegiatan Tercatat</td><td>: <span class="badge bg-primary px-2.5">' + activities.length + ' Transaksi</span></td></tr>' +
                '</table>' +
              '</div>' +
            '</div>' +
          '</div>' +

          '<div class="mt-4">' +
            '<div class="d-flex justify-content-between align-items-center mb-3">' +
              '<h6 class="fw-bold text-dark mb-0"><i class="fa-solid fa-clock-rotate-left text-primary me-2"></i>Riwayat & Historis Kegiatan Kepegawaian</h6>' +
              '<span class="badge bg-light text-secondary border">Total: ' + activities.length + ' Kegiatan</span>' +
            '</div>';

      if (activities.length === 0) {
        html += '<div class="text-center py-4 bg-light rounded-3 text-muted small"><i class="fa-solid fa-folder-open me-1"></i> Belum ada kegiatan kepegawaian yang tercatat untuk pegawai ini.</div>';
      } else {
        html += '<div class="table-responsive border rounded-3"><table class="table table-custom table-hover align-middle mb-0"><thead><tr><th>TANGGAL</th><th>JENIS KEGIATAN BAKU</th><th>JABATAN LAMA</th><th>UNIT KERJA LAMA</th><th>JABATAN BARU</th><th>UNIT KERJA BARU</th><th>NOMOR SK / SURAT</th><th>TMT</th><th>WAKTU INPUT</th><th class="text-end no-print">AKSI</th></tr></thead><tbody>';

        activities.forEach(function(act) {
          var fileBtn = act.fileData ? '<button class="btn btn-sm btn-outline-secondary me-1" title="Lihat Berkas SK" onclick="previewUploadedDoc(\'' + escJs(act.kegiatanID) + '\')"><i class="fa-solid fa-paperclip text-primary"></i></button>' : '';

          html += '<tr>' +
            '<td><span class="badge bg-light text-dark border">' + safeDate(act.tanggalKegiatan) + '</span></td>' +
            '<td><strong class="text-dark d-block">' + esc(act.namaJenisKegiatan) + '</strong><small class="text-muted">ID: ' + esc(act.kegiatanID) + '</small></td>' +
            '<td><small class="text-dark">' + esc(act.jabatanLama || '-') + '</small></td>' +
            '<td><small class="text-muted">' + esc(act.unitKerjaLama || '-') + '</small></td>' +
            '<td><small class="text-dark fw-semibold">' + esc(act.jabatanBaru || '-') + '</small></td>' +
            '<td><small class="text-muted">' + esc(act.unitKerjaBaru || '-') + '</small></td>' +
            '<td><div><small class="text-dark d-block">SK: ' + esc(act.nomorSK || '-') + '</small>' + (act.tanggalSK ? '<small class="text-muted d-block">' + safeDate(act.tanggalSK) + '</small>' : '') + '</div></td>' +
            '<td><small class="fw-semibold text-primary">' + safeDate(act.tmt) + '</small></td>' +
            '<td>' + fmtDateTimeHtml(act.createdAt) + '</td>' +
            '<td class="text-end no-print">' +
              fileBtn +
              '<button class="btn btn-sm btn-light border text-primary me-1" onclick="openRiwayatEdit(\'' + escJs(act.kegiatanID) + '\')"><i class="fa-solid fa-pen-to-square"></i> Edit</button>' +
              '<button class="btn btn-sm btn-light border text-danger" title="Hapus" onclick="deleteKegiatanAction(\'' + escJs(act.kegiatanID) + '\')"><i class="fa-solid fa-trash"></i></button>' +
            '</td>' +
          '</tr>';
        });

        html += '</tbody></table></div>';
      }

      html += '</div></div>';
      container.innerHTML = html;
    }

    function selectPegawaiForKegiatan(pegawaiID) {
      var p = dbPegawai.find(function(x){ return x.pegawaiID === pegawaiID; });
      if (!p) return;

      if (selectPegawaiTargetContext === 'profil') {
        selectedProfilPegawaiID = p.pegawaiID;
        showPage('profil');
        var modalEl = document.getElementById('selectPegawaiModal');
        var modalInst = bootstrap.Modal.getInstance(modalEl);
        if (modalInst) modalInst.hide();
        return;
      }

      var fullName = (p.gelarDepan ? p.gelarDepan + ' ' : '') + p.nama + (p.gelarBelakang ? ', ' + p.gelarBelakang : '');
      var nipCode = isNipStatus(p.statusPegawai) ? p.nip : p.nipppk;

      document.getElementById('page_input_pegawai_id').value = p.pegawaiID;
      document.getElementById('page_input_nama').value = fullName;
      document.getElementById('page_input_nip').value = nipCode || '';
      document.getElementById('page_input_pangkat_gol').value = pangkatGolText(p);
      document.getElementById('page_input_jabatan_lama').value = p.jabatan || '';
      document.getElementById('page_input_unit_kerja_lama').value = (p.unor ? p.unor + ' - ' : '') + (p.unitInduk || '');

      var modalEl = document.getElementById('selectPegawaiModal');
      var modalInst = bootstrap.Modal.getInstance(modalEl);
      if (modalInst) modalInst.hide();
    }

    function kegLabel(item) { return (item.nama || '-') + ' - ' + (item.namaJenisKegiatan || ''); }


    function dateVal(v) { var d = safeDate(v); return d === '-' ? '' : d; }

    function openRiwayatEdit(kegiatanID) {
      var item = dbKegiatan.find(function(x) { return x.kegiatanID === kegiatanID; });
      if (!item) return;
      requireIzin('EDIT_KEGIATAN', kegiatanID, kegLabel(item)).then(function(ok) {
        if (!ok) return;
        var set = function(id, v) { document.getElementById(id).value = v == null ? '' : v; };
        set('rw_id', item.kegiatanID); set('rw_file_data', item.fileData || '');
        set('rw_tanggal', dateVal(item.tanggalKegiatan)); set('rw_tmt', dateVal(item.tmt));
        set('rw_pangkat', item.pangkatGol); set('rw_jab_lama', item.jabatanLama); set('rw_jab_baru', item.jabatanBaru);
        set('rw_unit_lama', item.unitKerjaLama); set('rw_unit_baru', item.unitKerjaBaru);
        set('rw_no_sk', item.nomorSK); set('rw_tgl_sk', dateVal(item.tanggalSK)); set('rw_ket', item.keterangan);
        document.getElementById('rw_info').innerHTML = '<strong>' + esc(item.nama || '-') + '</strong> &middot; ' + esc(item.namaJenisKegiatan || '-') + ' <span class="text-muted">(' + esc(item.kegiatanID) + ')</span>';
        document.getElementById('rw_file_info').textContent = item.fileData ? 'Berkas SK sudah terlampir. Pilih file baru hanya jika ingin menggantinya.' : 'PDF, PNG, JPG. Maksimal 1 MB.';
        bootstrap.Modal.getOrCreateInstance(document.getElementById('riwayatEditModal')).show();
      });
    }

    function submitRiwayatEdit() {
      var id = document.getElementById('rw_id').value;
      var idx = dbKegiatan.findIndex(function(x) { return x.kegiatanID === id; });
      if (idx === -1) return;
      var g = function(i) { return document.getElementById(i).value.trim(); };
      if (!document.getElementById('rw_tanggal').value || !document.getElementById('rw_tmt').value) return Swal.fire('Peringatan', 'Tanggal Kegiatan dan TMT wajib diisi!', 'warning');
      var old = dbKegiatan[idx];
      var payload = Object.assign({}, old, {
        tanggalKegiatan: g('rw_tanggal'), tmt: g('rw_tmt'), pangkatGol: g('rw_pangkat'),
        jabatanLama: g('rw_jab_lama'), jabatanBaru: g('rw_jab_baru'),
        unitKerjaLama: g('rw_unit_lama'), unitKerjaBaru: g('rw_unit_baru'),
        nomorSK: g('rw_no_sk'), tanggalSK: document.getElementById('rw_tgl_sk').value, keterangan: g('rw_ket'),
        fileData: document.getElementById('rw_file_data').value
      });
      delete payload._isSyncing;
      bootstrap.Modal.getOrCreateInstance(document.getElementById('riwayatEditModal')).hide();

      var backup = JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(dbKegiatan));
      dbKegiatan[idx] = Object.assign({}, old, payload, { fileData: payload.fileData.indexOf('data:') === 0 ? 'data-baru' : payload.fileData, _isSyncing: true });
      renderProfilPegawaiView();

      if (typeof google === 'undefined') {
        setTimeout(function() {
          delete dbKegiatan[idx]._isSyncing; renderProfilPegawaiView();
          Swal.fire({ icon: 'success', title: 'Riwayat Diperbarui (Simulasi)', toast: true, position: 'top-end', showConfirmButton: false, timer: 1600 });
        }, 500);
        return;
      }
      gasRun('saveDataKegiatan', payload).then(function(res) {
        if (res.success) {
          fetchKegiatanData(true); fetchPegawaiData(true); refreshIzin();
          Swal.fire({ icon: 'success', title: 'Riwayat Diperbarui', toast: true, position: 'top-end', showConfirmButton: false, timer: 1600 });
        } else { dbKegiatan = backup; renderProfilPegawaiView(); Swal.fire('Gagal', res.message, 'error'); }
      }).catch(function(err) { dbKegiatan = backup; renderProfilPegawaiView(); Swal.fire('Error', err.message || 'Gagal menyimpan', 'error'); refreshIzin(); });
    }

    function editKegiatan(kegiatanID) {
      var item = dbKegiatan.find(function(x){ return x.kegiatanID === kegiatanID; });
      if (!item) return;
      requireIzin('EDIT_KEGIATAN', kegiatanID, kegLabel(item)).then(function(ok) { if (ok) openEditKegiatanForm(kegiatanID); });
    }

    function openEditKegiatanForm(kegiatanID) {
      var item = dbKegiatan.find(function(x){ return x.kegiatanID === kegiatanID; });
      if (!item) return;

      showPage('input');

      document.getElementById('page_input_kegiatan_id').value = item.kegiatanID;
      document.getElementById('page_input_pegawai_id').value = item.pegawaiID || '';
      document.getElementById('page_input_file_data').value = item.fileData || '';
      document.getElementById('page_input_tanggal').value = safeDate(item.tanggalKegiatan);
      document.getElementById('page_input_nama').value = item.nama || '';
      document.getElementById('page_input_nip').value = item.nip || '';
      document.getElementById('page_input_pangkat_gol').value = item.pangkatGol || '';
      document.getElementById('page_input_pangkat_baru').value = item.pangkatGolBaru || '';
      (function() {
        var sj = document.getElementById('page_input_jenis_jabatan'), v = item.jenisJabatan || '';
        if (v && ![].some.call(sj.options, function(o) { return o.value === v || o.text === v; })) { var o = document.createElement('option'); o.text = v; sj.add(o); }
        sj.value = v;
      })();
      document.getElementById('page_input_kelas_jabatan').value = item.kelasJabatan || '';
      document.getElementById('page_input_kelas_jabatan_lama').value = item.kelasJabatanLama || '';
      document.getElementById('page_input_jabatan_lama').value = item.jabatanLama || '';
      document.getElementById('page_input_jabatan_baru').value = item.jabatanBaru || '';
      document.getElementById('page_input_unit_kerja_lama').value = item.unitKerjaLama || '';
      document.getElementById('page_input_unit_kerja_baru').value = item.unitKerjaBaru || '';
      document.getElementById('page_input_instansi_asal').value = item.instansiAsal || 'Pemerintah Kabupaten Pasuruan';
      document.getElementById('page_input_instansi_tujuan').value = item.instansiTujuan || 'Pemerintah Kabupaten Pasuruan';
      document.getElementById('page_input_nomor_usulan').value = item.nomorUsulan || '';
      document.getElementById('page_input_tanggal_usulan').value = safeDate(item.tanggalUsulan);
      document.getElementById('page_input_nomor_sk').value = item.nomorSK || '';
      document.getElementById('page_input_tanggal_sk').value = safeDate(item.tanggalSK);
      document.getElementById('page_input_tmt').value = safeDate(item.tmt);
      document.getElementById('page_input_keterangan').value = item.keterangan || '';

      var infoEl = document.getElementById('fileUploadInfo');
      if (infoEl) {
        infoEl.innerHTML = item.fileData ? '<span class="text-success fw-bold"><i class="fa-solid fa-paperclip me-1"></i> File SK Terlampir</span>' : 'Format didukung: PDF, PNG, JPG. Maksimal 1 MB.';
      }

      updateInputTimeInfo();
      var selectJenis = document.getElementById('page_input_jenis_kegiatan');
      for (var i = 0; i < selectJenis.options.length; i++) {
        if (selectJenis.options[i].value.includes(item.namaJenisKegiatan)) {
          selectJenis.selectedIndex = i; break;
        }
      }
      document.getElementById('page_input_tt_lama').value = item.tugasTambahanLama || '';
      document.getElementById('page_input_tt_baru').value = item.tugasTambahanBaru || '';
      try { onJenisTugasTambahan(); applyKenaikanPangkatMode(); applyKgbMode(); } catch (e) {}
      document.getElementById('page_input_pangkat_baru').value = item.pangkatGolBaru || '';
      try { var _jk = document.getElementById('page_input_jumlah_kgb'); _jk.value = item.jumlahKGB || ''; } catch (e) {}
      try { updatePltCalc(); } catch (e) {}
    }

    function deleteKegiatanAction(kegiatanID) {
      var it = dbKegiatan.find(function(x){ return x.kegiatanID === kegiatanID; });
      requireIzin('HAPUS_KEGIATAN', kegiatanID, it ? kegLabel(it) : kegiatanID).then(function(ok) { if (ok) doDeleteKegiatan(kegiatanID); });
    }

    function doDeleteKegiatan(kegiatanID) {
      Swal.fire({ title: 'Hapus Kegiatan?', text: 'Data ' + kegiatanID + ' akan dihapus.', icon: 'warning', showCancelButton: true, confirmButtonColor: '#d33', confirmButtonText: 'Ya, Hapus' }).then(function(res) {
        if (!res.isConfirmed) return;

        var backupData = JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(dbKegiatan));
        dbKegiatan = dbKegiatan.filter(function(x){ return x.kegiatanID !== kegiatanID; });
        renderDataKegiatanTable();
        if (activePage === 'profil') renderProfilPegawaiView();
        Swal.fire({ icon: 'success', title: 'Kegiatan Dihapus', toast: true, position: 'top-end', showConfirmButton: false, timer: 1500 });

        if (typeof google !== 'undefined') {
          gasRun('deleteDataKegiatan', kegiatanID).then(function(r) {
            if (r.success) fetchPegawaiData(true);
            if (!r.success) { dbKegiatan = backupData; renderDataKegiatanTable(); if (activePage === 'profil') renderProfilPegawaiView(); Swal.fire('Error', r.message, 'error'); }
            refreshIzin();
          }).catch(function(err) { dbKegiatan = backupData; renderDataKegiatanTable(); if (activePage === 'profil') renderProfilPegawaiView(); Swal.fire('Error', err.message, 'error'); refreshIzin(); });
        }
      });
    }

    function applyRekapPeriodeQuickFilter() {
      var p = document.getElementById('filterRekapPeriode')?.value || 'ALL';
      var yEl = document.getElementById('filterRekapTahun');
      var y = (yEl && yEl.value) || '';
      var startEl = document.getElementById('filterRekapStart');
      var endEl = document.getElementById('filterRekapEnd');
      if (p === 'ALL') {
        // tanpa periode cepat: Tahun menyaring satu tahun penuh; "Semua Tahun" = tanpa batas tanggal
        if (startEl) startEl.value = y ? y + '-01-01' : '';
        if (endEl) endEl.value = y ? y + '-12-31' : '';
        renderRekapitulasiView();
        if (activePage === 'dokumen') renderDokumenLaporanView();
        return;
      }
      if (!y) { y = String(new Date().getFullYear()); if (yEl) yEl.value = y; }

      if (p === 'Q1') {
        if (startEl) startEl.value = y + '-01-01';
        if (endEl) endEl.value = y + '-03-31';
      } else if (p === 'Q2') {
        if (startEl) startEl.value = y + '-04-01';
        if (endEl) endEl.value = y + '-06-30';
      } else if (p === 'Q3') {
        if (startEl) startEl.value = y + '-07-01';
        if (endEl) endEl.value = y + '-09-30';
      } else if (p === 'Q4') {
        if (startEl) startEl.value = y + '-10-01';
        if (endEl) endEl.value = y + '-12-31';
      } else if (p === 'S1') {
        if (startEl) startEl.value = y + '-01-01';
        if (endEl) endEl.value = y + '-06-30';
      } else if (p === 'S2') {
        if (startEl) startEl.value = y + '-07-01';
        if (endEl) endEl.value = y + '-12-31';
      } else if (p === 'YEAR') {
        if (startEl) startEl.value = y + '-01-01';
        if (endEl) endEl.value = y + '-12-31';
      }

      renderRekapitulasiView();
      if (activePage === 'dokumen') renderDokumenLaporanView();
    }

    var rekapOpen = {};
    var rekapKeysWithData = [];

    function toggleRekapDetail(key) {
      rekapOpen[key] = !rekapOpen[key];
      renderRekapitulasiView();
    }

    function toggleRekapAll() {
      var allOpen = rekapKeysWithData.length > 0 && rekapKeysWithData.every(function(k) { return rekapOpen[k]; });
      rekapKeysWithData.forEach(function(k) { rekapOpen[k] = !allOpen; });
      renderRekapitulasiView();
    }

    function rekapArrow(a, b) {
      a = (a || '').trim(); b = (b || '').trim();
      if (a && b && a !== b) return esc(a) + ' <span class="text-primary">&rarr;</span> ' + esc(b);
      return esc(b || a || '-');
    }

    function rekapJabUnor(jab, unor, kelas, strong) {
      var j = esc(jab || '-');
      return '<span class="d-block' + (strong ? ' fw-semibold text-dark' : '') + '">' + j + '</span>' +
        '<span class="d-block text-muted">Unor: ' + esc(unor || '-') + '</span>' +
        (kelas ? '<span class="d-block text-muted">Kelas: ' + esc(kelas) + '</span>' : '');
    }
    function buildRekapDetailTable(records, hideInput) {
      var rows = records.slice().sort(function(x, y) {
        return String(safeDate(x.tanggalKegiatan)).localeCompare(String(safeDate(y.tanggalKegiatan)));
      });
      var hasTT = rows.some(function(r) {
        return r.tugasTambahanLama || r.tugasTambahanBaru || /kepala (sekolah|puskesmas)/i.test(r.namaJenisKegiatan || '');
      });
      var t = '<div class="table-responsive"><table class="table table-sm table-bordered align-middle small mb-0 bg-white">' +
        '<thead class="table-light"><tr>' +
          '<th class="text-center" style="width:36px;">No</th><th style="width:92px;">Tanggal</th><th>Nama / NIP</th><th>Jenis Kegiatan Baku</th>' +
          '<th>Jabatan Lama &amp; Unor Lama</th><th>Jabatan Baru &amp; Unor Baru</th>' + (hasTT ? '<th>Jabatan Tugas Tambahan Lama</th><th>Jabatan Tugas Tambahan Baru</th>' : '') + '<th>No. SK / Tgl SK</th>' +
          '<th style="width:92px;">TMT</th><th>Uraian / Keterangan</th>' + (hideInput ? '' : '<th style="width:104px;">Waktu Input</th>') +
        '</tr></thead><tbody>';
      rows.forEach(function(r, i) {
        t += '<tr>' +
          '<td class="text-center">' + (i + 1) + '</td>' +
          '<td>' + esc(safeDate(r.tanggalKegiatan)) + '</td>' +
          '<td><strong class="d-block text-dark">' + esc(r.nama || '-') + '</strong><span class="text-muted">' + esc(r.nip || '-') + '</span>' +
            (r.pangkatGol ? '<span class="d-block text-muted">' + esc(r.pangkatGol) + '</span>' : '') + '</td>' +
          '<td>' + esc(r.namaJenisKegiatan || '-') + '</td>' +
          '<td>' + rekapJabUnor(r.jabatanLama, r.unitKerjaLama, r.kelasJabatanLama) + '</td>' +
          '<td>' + rekapJabUnor(r.jabatanBaru, r.unitKerjaBaru, r.kelasJabatan, true) + '</td>' +
          (hasTT ? '<td>' + esc(r.tugasTambahanLama || '-') + '</td><td><strong>' + esc(r.tugasTambahanBaru || '-') + '</strong></td>' : '') +
          '<td>' + esc(r.nomorSK || '-') + (r.tanggalSK ? '<span class="d-block text-muted">' + esc(safeDate(r.tanggalSK)) + '</span>' : '') + '</td>' +
          '<td>' + esc(safeDate(r.tmt)) + '</td>' +
          '<td>' + esc(r.keterangan || '-') + '</td>' +
          (hideInput ? '' : '<td>' + fmtDateTimeHtml(r.createdAt) + '</td>') +
        '</tr>';
      });
      return t + '</tbody></table></div>';
    }


    // ===== CETAK REKAPITULASI PER JENIS KEGIATAN =====
    function buildRekapPrintSheet(m, list, st) {
      var today = new Date().toLocaleDateString('id-ID', { day: 'numeric', month: 'long', year: 'numeric' });
      var pct = st.grandTotal > 0 ? ((list.length / st.grandTotal) * 100).toFixed(1) : '0';
      var sign = penandatanganData || {};
      return '<div class="rp-sheet">' +
        '<div style="display:flex;align-items:center;gap:12px;border-bottom:3px double #000;padding-bottom:8px;margin-bottom:10px;">' +
          '<img src="' + esc(currentLogo) + '" alt="" style="width:62px;height:62px;object-fit:contain;">' +
          '<div style="text-align:center;flex:1;line-height:1.3;">' +
            '<div style="font-weight:700;font-size:12pt;">' + esc((sign.instansi || 'PEMERINTAH KABUPATEN PASURUAN').toUpperCase()) + '</div>' +
            '<div style="font-weight:700;font-size:11pt;">BADAN KEPEGAWAIAN DAN PENGEMBANGAN SUMBER DAYA MANUSIA</div>' +
            '<div style="font-size:9pt;">Sistem Informasi Arsip Mutasi Promosi (SIAMP)</div>' +
          '</div>' +
        '</div>' +
        '<div style="text-align:center;font-weight:700;font-size:12pt;margin-bottom:2px;">REKAPITULASI KEGIATAN KEPEGAWAIAN</div>' +
        '<div style="text-align:center;font-size:10.5pt;margin-bottom:8px;"><strong>' + esc(m.jenisKegiatanID) + ' &ndash; ' + esc(m.namaJenisKegiatan) + '</strong></div>' +
        '<table style="margin-bottom:8px;border:0;font-size:9.5pt;"><tr>' +
          '<td style="border:0!important;width:50%;">' + esc(st.periodText) + '</td>' +
          '<td style="border:0!important;text-align:right;">Jumlah: <strong>' + list.length + ' kegiatan</strong> (' + pct + '% dari ' + st.grandTotal + ' data)</td>' +
        '</tr></table>' +
        buildRekapDetailTable(list, true) +
        '<div style="margin-top:18px;float:right;text-align:center;font-size:10pt;min-width:260px;page-break-inside:avoid;">' +
          '<div>' + esc(sign.kota || 'Pasuruan') + ', ' + today + '</div>' +
          '<div style="font-weight:600;">' + esc(sign.jabatanPejabat || 'Kepala BKPSDM Kabupaten Pasuruan') + '</div>' +
          '<div style="height:56px;"></div>' +
          '<div style="font-weight:700;text-decoration:underline;">' + esc(sign.namaPejabat || '') + '</div>' +
          (sign.pangkatGol ? '<div>' + esc(sign.pangkatGol) + '</div>' : '') +
          '<div>NIP. ' + esc(sign.nipPejabat || '') + '</div>' +
        '</div>' +
        '<div style="clear:both;"></div>' +
      '</div>';
    }

    function doPrintRekap(sheetsHtml) {
      var area = document.getElementById('rekapPrintArea');
      if (!area) return;
      area.innerHTML = sheetsHtml;
      var st = document.createElement('style');
      st.id = 'rekapPageStyle';
      st.textContent = '@page { size: A4 landscape; margin: 12mm; }';
      document.head.appendChild(st);
      document.body.classList.add('printing-rekap');
      var cleanup = function() {
        document.body.classList.remove('printing-rekap');
        var el = document.getElementById('rekapPageStyle'); if (el) el.remove();
        area.innerHTML = '';
        window.removeEventListener('afterprint', cleanup);
      };
      window.addEventListener('afterprint', cleanup);
      setTimeout(function() { window.print(); setTimeout(function() { if (document.body.classList.contains('printing-rekap')) cleanup(); }, 1500); }, 150);
    }

    function printRekapJenis(jenisID) {
      var st = rekapPrintState;
      if (!st) return;
      var m = masterKegiatanList.find(function(x) { return x.jenisKegiatanID === jenisID; });
      if (!m) return;
      var list = (st.recordsByJenis[m.namaJenisKegiatan] || []);
      if (!list.length) return Swal.fire('Informasi', 'Tidak ada data pada jenis kegiatan ini.', 'info');
      doPrintRekap(buildRekapPrintSheet(m, list, st));
    }

    function printRekapSemuaJenis() {
      var st = rekapPrintState;
      if (!st) return;
      var html = '';
      st.displayMaster.forEach(function(m) {
        var list = st.recordsByJenis[m.namaJenisKegiatan] || [];
        if (list.length) html += buildRekapPrintSheet(m, list, st);
      });
      if (!html) return Swal.fire('Informasi', 'Tidak ada data untuk dicetak pada filter ini.', 'info');
      doPrintRekap(html);
    }


    // ===== EKSPOR REKAPITULASI PER JENIS (EXCEL & WORD) =====
    function exportRekapJenis(jenisID, fmt) {
      var st = rekapPrintState;
      if (!st) return;
      var items = [];
      st.displayMaster.forEach(function(m) {
        if (jenisID !== 'ALL' && m.jenisKegiatanID !== jenisID) return;
        var list = st.recordsByJenis[m.namaJenisKegiatan] || [];
        if (list.length) items.push({ m: m, list: list });
      });
      if (!items.length) return Swal.fire('Informasi', 'Tidak ada data untuk diekspor.', 'info');
      var stamp = new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10);
      var base = 'Rekapitulasi_' + (jenisID === 'ALL' ? 'Semua_Jenis' : jenisID) + '_' + stamp;
      if (fmt === 'xlsx') {
        if (typeof XLSX === 'undefined') return Swal.fire('Gagal', 'Library Excel belum termuat. Muat ulang halaman.', 'error');
        var wb = XLSX.utils.book_new();
        var used = {};
        items.forEach(function(it) {
          var rows = [['REKAPITULASI KEGIATAN KEPEGAWAIAN'], [it.m.jenisKegiatanID + ' - ' + it.m.namaJenisKegiatan], [st.periodText], [],
            ['No', 'Tanggal', 'Nama', 'NIP', 'Status', 'Pangkat/Gol', 'Jabatan Lama', 'Jabatan Baru', 'Tugas Tambahan Lama', 'Tugas Tambahan Baru', 'Unit Kerja Lama', 'Unit Kerja Baru', 'No. SK', 'Tgl SK', 'TMT', 'Keterangan']];
          it.list.forEach(function(x, i) {
            rows.push([i + 1, x.tanggalKegiatan || '', x.nama || '', x.nip ? String(x.nip) : '', x.statusPegawai || '', x.pangkatGol || '', x.jabatanLama || '', x.jabatanBaru || '', x.tugasTambahanLama || '', x.tugasTambahanBaru || '', x.unitKerjaLama || '', x.unitKerjaBaru || '', x.nomorSK || '', x.tanggalSK || '', x.tmt || '', x.keterangan || '']);
          });
          var ws = XLSX.utils.aoa_to_sheet(rows);
          ws['!cols'] = [6, 12, 28, 20, 8, 20, 28, 28, 24, 24, 28, 28, 24, 12, 12, 36].map(function(w) { return { wch: w }; });
          var nm = (it.m.jenisKegiatanID + ' ' + it.m.namaJenisKegiatan).replace(/[\\\/\?\*\[\]:]/g, ' ').slice(0, 28);
          var k = nm, n = 1; while (used[k]) { k = nm.slice(0, 26) + '_' + (++n); }
          used[k] = true;
          XLSX.utils.book_append_sheet(wb, ws, k);
        });
        XLSX.writeFile(wb, base + '.xlsx');
      } else {
        var body = items.map(function(it) { return buildRekapPrintSheet(it.m, it.list, st); }).join('<br clear="all" style="page-break-before:always">');
        var html = '<html xmlns:o="urn:schemas-microsoft-com:office:office" xmlns:w="urn:schemas-microsoft-com:office:word" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/TR/REC-html40"><head><meta charset="utf-8"><title>Rekapitulasi</title>' +
          '<style>@page{size:29.7cm 21cm;mso-page-orientation:landscape;margin:1.2cm}body{font-family:Arial,sans-serif;font-size:10pt}table{border-collapse:collapse;width:100%}th,td{border:1px solid #444;padding:3px 5px;vertical-align:top;font-size:8.5pt}th{background:#eee}</style></head><body>' + body + '</body></html>';
        var blob = new Blob(['﻿', html], { type: 'application/msword' });
        var a = document.createElement('a');
        a.href = URL.createObjectURL(blob); a.download = base + '.doc';
        document.body.appendChild(a); a.click();
        setTimeout(function() { URL.revokeObjectURL(a.href); a.remove(); }, 500);
      }
    }

    var rekapPrintState = null;
    var recordsByJenisAll = {};

    function renderRekapitulasiView() {
      var container = document.getElementById('rekapContainer');
      if (!container) return;

      var search = (document.getElementById('searchRekapInput')?.value || '').toLowerCase();
      var filterStatus = document.getElementById('filterRekapStatus')?.value || '';
      var filterUnit = document.getElementById('filterRekapUnitInduk')?.value || '';
      var dateStart = document.getElementById('filterRekapStart')?.value || '';
      var dateEnd = document.getElementById('filterRekapEnd')?.value || '';
      var pVal = document.getElementById('filterRekapPeriode')?.value || 'ALL';
      var yVal = document.getElementById('filterRekapTahun')?.value || '';

      var pegUnitMap = {};
      dbPegawai.forEach(function(p){ pegUnitMap[p.pegawaiID] = p.unitInduk; });

      var filteredData = dbKegiatan.filter(function(x) {
        if (filterStatus && x.statusPegawai !== filterStatus) return false;
        if (filterUnit) {
          var u = pegUnitMap[x.pegawaiID] || '';
          if (u !== filterUnit) return false;
        }
        if (dateStart || dateEnd) {
          var tgl = safeDate(x.tanggalKegiatan);
          if (!tgl || tgl === '-') return false;
          if (dateStart && tgl < dateStart) return false;
          if (dateEnd && tgl > dateEnd) return false;
        }
        return true;
      });

      var countMap = {};
      masterKegiatanList.forEach(function(m) { countMap[m.namaJenisKegiatan] = 0; });
      var grandTotal = 0;

      filteredData.forEach(function(x) {
        if (x.namaJenisKegiatan) {
          countMap[x.namaJenisKegiatan] = (countMap[x.namaJenisKegiatan] || 0) + 1;
          grandTotal += 1;
        }
      });

      var sortedMaster = masterKegiatanList.slice().sort(function(a,b){ return a.urutan - b.urutan; });
      var displayMaster = sortedMaster.filter(function(m) {
        return search === '' || m.namaJenisKegiatan.toLowerCase().includes(search);
      });

      var pLabelMap = {
        'Q1': 'Triwulan 1 (Jan - Mar)',
        'Q2': 'Triwulan 2 (Apr - Jun)',
        'Q3': 'Triwulan 3 (Jul - Sep)',
        'Q4': 'Triwulan 4 (Okt - Des)',
        'S1': 'Semester 1 (Jan - Jun)',
        'S2': 'Semester 2 (Jul - Des)',
        'YEAR': '1 Tahun Penuh'
      };

      var periodText = '';
      if (pVal !== 'ALL' && pLabelMap[pVal]) {
        periodText = 'Periode: ' + pLabelMap[pVal] + ' (' + yVal + ')';
      } else if (dateStart || dateEnd) {
        periodText = 'Periode: ' + esc(dateStart || 'Awal') + ' s.d ' + (dateEnd || 'Akhir');
      } else {
        periodText = 'Seluruh Periode Data';
      }

      var html = '<div class="card card-custom border-0 p-3 mb-3 bg-white">' +
        '<div class="d-flex justify-content-between align-items-center flex-wrap gap-2">' +
          '<div>' +
            '<span class="badge bg-primary-subtle text-primary border px-2.5 py-1 fw-bold">' + esc(periodText) + '</span>' +
          '</div>' +
          '<div class="fw-bold text-dark fs-6">' +
            'Total Keseluruhan Kegiatan: <span class="badge bg-primary px-3 py-1.5 fs-6">' + grandTotal + ' Data</span>' +
          '</div>' +
        '</div>' +
      '</div>';

      if (displayMaster.length === 0) {
        html += '<div class="text-center py-5 bg-white rounded border">' +
          '<i class="fa-solid fa-magnifying-glass fa-2x text-muted mb-2"></i>' +
          '<p class="text-muted mb-0">Tidak ada jenis kegiatan yang cocok dengan kata kunci pencarian.</p>' +
        '</div>';
      } else {
        html += '<div class="card card-custom border-0 overflow-hidden">' +
          '<div class="table-responsive">' +
            '<table class="table table-custom table-hover align-middle mb-0">' +
              '<thead>' +
                '<tr>' +
                  '<th style="width: 70px;" class="text-center">No</th>' +
                  '<th>Nama Jenis Kegiatan Kepegawaian</th>' +
                  '<th style="width: 180px;" class="text-end">Jumlah Kegiatan</th>' +
                  '<th style="width: 140px;" class="text-end">Persentase</th>' +
                '</tr>' +
              '</thead>' +
              '<tbody>';

        var recordsByJenis = {};
        recordsByJenisAll = recordsByJenis;
        filteredData.forEach(function(x) {
          if (!x.namaJenisKegiatan) return;
          (recordsByJenis[x.namaJenisKegiatan] = recordsByJenis[x.namaJenisKegiatan] || []).push(x);
        });
        rekapKeysWithData = displayMaster
          .filter(function(m) { return (countMap[m.namaJenisKegiatan] || 0) > 0; })
          .map(function(m) { return m.jenisKegiatanID; });

        displayMaster.forEach(function(m) {
          var count = countMap[m.namaJenisKegiatan] || 0;
          var pct = grandTotal > 0 ? ((count / grandTotal) * 100).toFixed(1) : '0';
          var badgeStyle = count > 0 ? 'bg-primary-subtle text-primary border' : 'bg-light text-muted border';
          var isOpen = count > 0 && !!rekapOpen[m.jenisKegiatanID];

          var toggle = count > 0
            ? '<button type="button" class="btn btn-sm btn-light border me-2 no-print" title="Lihat uraian" onclick="toggleRekapDetail(\'' + escJs(m.jenisKegiatanID) + '\')"><i class="fa-solid ' + (isOpen ? 'fa-chevron-down' : 'fa-chevron-right') + '"></i></button>'
            : '<span class="d-inline-block me-2 no-print" style="width:34px;"></span>';

          var printBtn = count > 0
            ? '<button type="button" class="btn btn-sm btn-outline-secondary ms-2 no-print" title="Cetak uraian jenis kegiatan ini" onclick="printRekapJenis(\'' + escJs(m.jenisKegiatanID) + '\')"><i class="fa-solid fa-print"></i> Cetak</button>' +
              '<button type="button" class="btn btn-sm btn-outline-success ms-1 no-print" title="Unduh Excel" onclick="exportRekapJenis(\'' + escJs(m.jenisKegiatanID) + '\',\'xlsx\')"><i class="fa-solid fa-file-excel"></i></button>'
            : '';
          html += '<tr>' +
            '<td class="text-center fw-bold">' + esc(m.urutan) + '</td>' +
            '<td>' + toggle + '<strong class="text-dark">' + esc(m.namaJenisKegiatan) + '</strong>' + printBtn + '</td>' +
            '<td class="text-end"><span class="badge ' + badgeStyle + ' px-3 py-1.5 fs-6 fw-semibold">' + count + ' Data</span></td>' +
            '<td class="text-end text-muted fw-semibold">' + pct + '%</td>' +
          '</tr>';

          if (isOpen) {
            html += '<tr class="rekap-detail"><td></td><td colspan="3" class="bg-light p-3">' +
              '<div class="fw-semibold small mb-2 text-dark"><i class="fa-solid fa-list-ul text-primary me-1"></i>Uraian ' + count + ' kegiatan:</div>' +
              buildRekapDetailTable(recordsByJenis[m.namaJenisKegiatan] || []) +
            '</td></tr>';
          }
        });

        html += '</tbody>' +
            '</table>' +
          '</div>' +
        '</div>';
      }

      rekapPrintState = { recordsByJenis: recordsByJenisAll, periodText: periodText, grandTotal: grandTotal, displayMaster: displayMaster, countMap: countMap };
      container.innerHTML = html;

      var btnAll = document.getElementById('btnRekapToggleAll');
      if (btnAll) {
        var allOpen = rekapKeysWithData.length > 0 && rekapKeysWithData.every(function(k) { return rekapOpen[k]; });
        btnAll.innerHTML = allOpen
          ? '<i class="fa-solid fa-angles-up me-1"></i> Tutup Semua Uraian'
          : '<i class="fa-solid fa-angles-down me-1"></i> Buka Semua Uraian';
        btnAll.disabled = rekapKeysWithData.length === 0;
      }
    }

    var docReportChartInstance = null;

    function applyDocPeriodeQuickFilter() {
      var p = document.getElementById('docFilterPeriode')?.value || 'ALL';
      var yEl = document.getElementById('docFilterTahun');
      var y = (yEl && yEl.value) || '';
      var startEl = document.getElementById('docFilterStart');
      var endEl = document.getElementById('docFilterEnd');
      if (p === 'ALL') {
        if (startEl) startEl.value = y ? y + '-01-01' : '';
        if (endEl) endEl.value = y ? y + '-12-31' : '';
        renderDokumenLaporanView();
        return;
      }
      if (!y) { y = String(new Date().getFullYear()); if (yEl) yEl.value = y; }

      if (p === 'Q1') {
        if (startEl) startEl.value = y + '-01-01';
        if (endEl) endEl.value = y + '-03-31';
      } else if (p === 'Q2') {
        if (startEl) startEl.value = y + '-04-01';
        if (endEl) endEl.value = y + '-06-30';
      } else if (p === 'Q3') {
        if (startEl) startEl.value = y + '-07-01';
        if (endEl) endEl.value = y + '-09-30';
      } else if (p === 'Q4') {
        if (startEl) startEl.value = y + '-10-01';
        if (endEl) endEl.value = y + '-12-31';
      } else if (p === 'S1') {
        if (startEl) startEl.value = y + '-01-01';
        if (endEl) endEl.value = y + '-06-30';
      } else if (p === 'S2') {
        if (startEl) startEl.value = y + '-07-01';
        if (endEl) endEl.value = y + '-12-31';
      } else if (p === 'YEAR') {
        if (startEl) startEl.value = y + '-01-01';
        if (endEl) endEl.value = y + '-12-31';
      }

      renderDokumenLaporanView();
    }

    function uraianKegiatanHtml(r) {
      var lines = [];
      var jl = (r.jabatanLama || '').trim(), jb = (r.jabatanBaru || '').trim();
      var ul = (r.unitKerjaLama || '').trim(), ub = (r.unitKerjaBaru || '').trim();
      if (jl && jb && jl !== jb) lines.push('Jabatan dari <b>' + esc(jl) + '</b> menjadi <b>' + esc(jb) + '</b>');
      else if (jb || jl) lines.push('Jabatan: <b>' + esc(jb || jl) + '</b>');
      if (ul && ub && ul !== ub) lines.push('Unit kerja dari ' + esc(ul) + ' ke ' + esc(ub));
      else if (ub || ul) lines.push('Unit kerja: ' + esc(ub || ul));
      if (r.nomorUsulan) lines.push('Usulan No. ' + esc(r.nomorUsulan) + (r.tanggalUsulan ? ' tgl. ' + esc(safeDate(r.tanggalUsulan)) : ''));
      if (r.keterangan) lines.push('<i>' + esc(r.keterangan) + '</i>');
      return lines.length ? lines.join('<br>') : '-';
    }

    function buildDokumenUraian(records, masterSorted) {
      var byJenis = {};
      records.forEach(function(x) {
        if (x.namaJenisKegiatan) (byJenis[x.namaJenisKegiatan] = byJenis[x.namaJenisKegiatan] || []).push(x);
      });

      var out = '<div class="my-4 text-dark">' +
        '<h6 class="fw-bold text-dark border-bottom pb-1 mb-2" style="font-size:1rem;"><i class="fa-solid fa-list-ol text-primary me-2"></i>III. URAIAN KEGIATAN PER JENIS KEGIATAN KEPEGAWAIAN</h6>' +
        '<p class="small text-secondary mb-3" style="text-align: justify; line-height: 1.6;">Berikut diuraikan setiap jenis kegiatan kepegawaian yang tercatat pada periode laporan, meliputi pegawai yang bersangkutan, perubahan jabatan dan unit kerja, serta dasar surat keputusan dan TMT.</p>';

      var shown = 0;
      masterSorted.forEach(function(m) {
        var list = byJenis[m.namaJenisKegiatan];
        if (!list || !list.length) return;
        shown++;
        list = list.slice().sort(function(a, b) { return String(safeDate(a.tanggalKegiatan)).localeCompare(String(safeDate(b.tanggalKegiatan))); });
        var people = {};
        list.forEach(function(r) { people[r.nip || r.nama] = 1; });

        out += '<div class="mb-4" style="page-break-inside: avoid;">' +
          '<div class="fw-bold small mb-1">' + esc(m.urutan) + '. ' + esc(m.namaJenisKegiatan) + '</div>' +
          '<p class="small text-secondary mb-2" style="text-align: justify;">Tercatat <strong>' + list.length + ' kegiatan</strong> yang melibatkan <strong>' + Object.keys(people).length + ' pegawai</strong>, dengan rincian sebagai berikut:</p>' +
          buildRekapDetailTable(list, true) +
        '</div>';
      });

      if (!shown) out += '<p class="small text-muted fst-italic">Tidak ada kegiatan pada periode atau filter yang dipilih.</p>';
      return out + '</div>';
    }

    function renderDokumenLaporanView() {
      var container = document.getElementById('dokumenLaporanContainer');
      if (!container) return;

      var filterStatus = document.getElementById('docFilterStatus')?.value || '';
      var filterUnit = document.getElementById('docFilterUnit')?.value || '';
      var dateStart = document.getElementById('docFilterStart')?.value || '';
      var dateEnd = document.getElementById('docFilterEnd')?.value || '';
      var pVal = document.getElementById('docFilterPeriode')?.value || 'ALL';
      var yVal = document.getElementById('docFilterTahun')?.value || '';

      var pegUnitMap = {};
      dbPegawai.forEach(function(p){ pegUnitMap[p.pegawaiID] = p.unitInduk; });

      var filteredData = dbKegiatan.filter(function(x) {
        if (filterStatus && x.statusPegawai !== filterStatus) return false;
        if (filterUnit) {
          var u = pegUnitMap[x.pegawaiID] || '';
          if (u !== filterUnit) return false;
        }
        if (dateStart || dateEnd) {
          var tgl = safeDate(x.tanggalKegiatan);
          if (!tgl || tgl === '-') return false;
          if (dateStart && tgl < dateStart) return false;
          if (dateEnd && tgl > dateEnd) return false;
        }
        return true;
      });

      var countMap = {};
      masterKegiatanList.forEach(function(m) { countMap[m.namaJenisKegiatan] = 0; });
      var grandTotal = 0;

      filteredData.forEach(function(x) {
        if (x.namaJenisKegiatan) {
          countMap[x.namaJenisKegiatan] = (countMap[x.namaJenisKegiatan] || 0) + 1;
          grandTotal += 1;
        }
      });

      var topActivityName = '-';
      var topActivityCount = 0;
      Object.keys(countMap).forEach(function(k) {
        if (countMap[k] > topActivityCount) {
          topActivityCount = countMap[k];
          topActivityName = k;
        }
      });

      var pLabelMap = {
        'Q1': 'Triwulan 1 (Jan - Mar)',
        'Q2': 'Triwulan 2 (Apr - Jun)',
        'Q3': 'Triwulan 3 (Jul - Sep)',
        'Q4': 'Triwulan 4 (Okt - Des)',
        'S1': 'Semester 1 (Jan - Jun)',
        'S2': 'Semester 2 (Jul - Des)',
        'YEAR': '1 Tahun Penuh'
      };

      var periodText = '';
      if (pVal !== 'ALL' && pLabelMap[pVal]) {
        periodText = pLabelMap[pVal] + ' Tahun ' + yVal;
      } else if (dateStart || dateEnd) {
        periodText = (dateStart || 'Awal') + ' s/d ' + (dateEnd || 'Sekarang');
      } else {
        periodText = 'Seluruh Periode Data';
      }

      var todayFormatted = new Date().toLocaleDateString('id-ID', { day: 'numeric', month: 'long', year: 'numeric' });
      var uraianDocHtml = buildDokumenUraian(filteredData, masterKegiatanList.slice().sort(function(a, b) { return a.urutan - b.urutan; }));

      var html =
        '<div class="card card-custom border-0 shadow p-4 p-md-5 bg-white print-area mx-auto" style="max-width: 950px; font-family: \'Times New Roman\', serif;">' +
          '<div style="border-bottom: 3px solid #000; padding-bottom: 8px; margin-bottom: 20px;">' +
            '<table style="width: 100%; border: none;">' +
              '<tr>' +
                '<td style="width: 100px; text-align: center; vertical-align: middle; border: none;">' +
                  '<img src="' + currentLogo + '" alt="Logo Pemkab" style="max-width: 90px; max-height: 95px; width: auto; height: auto; object-fit: contain;">' +
                '</td>' +
                '<td style="text-align: center; vertical-align: middle; border: none; font-family: \'Times New Roman\', serif;">' +
                  '<div style="font-size: 14pt; font-weight: bold; letter-spacing: 0.5px; color: #000;">PEMERINTAH KABUPATEN PASURUAN</div>' +
                  '<div style="font-size: 16pt; font-weight: bold; letter-spacing: 0.5px; color: #000; line-height: 1.2;">BADAN KEPEGAWAIAN DAN PENGEMBANGAN</div>' +
                  '<div style="font-size: 16pt; font-weight: bold; letter-spacing: 0.5px; color: #000; line-height: 1.2; margin-bottom: 4px;">SUMBER DAYA MANUSIA</div>' +
                  '<div style="font-size: 10pt; color: #000;">Kompleks Perkantoran Pemerintah Kabupaten Pasuruan</div>' +
                  '<div style="font-size: 10pt; color: #000;">Jl. Raya Raci KM. 9, Bangil, Pasuruan</div>' +
                  '<div style="font-size: 9.5pt; color: #000;">Pos-el bkpsdm@pasuruankab.go.id, Website : https://bkpsdm.pasuruankab.go.id</div>' +
                '</td>' +
              '</tr>' +
            '</table>' +
          '</div>' +

          '<div class="text-center my-3">' +
            '<h5 class="fw-bold text-dark text-uppercase text-decoration-underline mb-1" style="font-family:\'Times New Roman\', serif;">DOKUMEN REKAPITULASI KEGIATAN KEPEGAWAIAN (SIAMP)</h5>' +
            '<p class="small text-muted mb-0">Periode: <strong>' + esc(periodText) + '</strong> | Status: <strong>' + esc(filterStatus || 'Semua Status') + '</strong> | Unit: <strong>' + esc(filterUnit || 'Semua Perangkat Daerah') + '</strong></p>' +
          '</div>' +

          '<div class="my-4 text-dark">' +
            '<h6 class="fw-bold text-dark border-bottom pb-1 mb-2" style="font-size:1rem;"><i class="fa-solid fa-book-open text-primary me-2"></i>I. PENDAHULUAN</h6>' +
            '<p class="small text-secondary mb-2" style="text-align: justify; line-height: 1.6;">' +
              'Laporan Rekapitulasi Kegiatan Kepegawaian ini disusun melalui Sistem Informasi Arsip Mutasi Promosi (SIAMP) sebagai instrumen pertanggungjawaban, evaluasi, dan pemantauan berkala terhadap pelaksanaan pelayanan administrasi mutasi, promosi, penetapan tugas tambahan, serta hak kepegawaian Aparatur Sipil Negara (ASN) di lingkungan Pemerintah Kabupaten Pasuruan.' +
            '</p>' +
          '</div>' +

          '<div class="my-4 text-dark">' +
            '<h6 class="fw-bold text-dark border-bottom pb-1 mb-3" style="font-size:1rem;"><i class="fa-solid fa-chart-pie text-primary me-2"></i>II. ISI DAN PEMBAHASAN (ANALISIS DATA)</h6>' +
            '<p class="small text-secondary mb-3" style="text-align: justify; line-height: 1.6;">' +
              'Berdasarkan data transaksi kepegawaian yang tercatat pada sistem SIAMP untuk periode <strong>' + esc(periodText) + '</strong>, teridentifikasi total sebanyak <strong>' + grandTotal + ' kegiatan kepegawaian</strong>. Dari keseluruhan layanan tersebut, kegiatan dengan volume terbanyak adalah <strong>' + esc(topActivityName) + '</strong> sebanyak <strong>' + topActivityCount + ' berkas/kegiatan</strong>.' +
            '</p>' +

            '<div class="my-4 p-3 bg-white rounded border">' +
              '<h6 class="fw-bold text-dark mb-3" style="font-size:0.9rem;"><i class="fa-solid fa-chart-column text-primary me-2"></i>Grafik Distribusi 40 Jenis Kegiatan Kepegawaian Baku</h6>' +
              '<div style="height: 380px; position: relative;">' +
                '<canvas id="docReportChart"></canvas>' +
              '</div>' +
            '</div>' +

            '<div class="table-responsive mb-4">' +
              '<table class="table table-bordered align-middle small mb-0">' +
                '<thead class="bg-light text-center">' +
                  '<tr>' +
                    '<th style="width: 50px;">NO</th>' +
                    '<th>NAMA JENIS KEGIATAN KEPEGAWAIAN BAKU</th>' +
                    '<th style="width: 150px;">JUMLAH DATA</th>' +
                    '<th style="width: 120px;">PERSENTASE</th>' +
                  '</tr>' +
                '</thead>' +
                '<tbody>';

      var sortedMaster = masterKegiatanList.slice().sort(function(a,b){ return a.urutan - b.urutan; });
      sortedMaster.forEach(function(m) {
        var count = countMap[m.namaJenisKegiatan] || 0;
        var pct = grandTotal > 0 ? ((count / grandTotal) * 100).toFixed(1) : '0';
        html += '<tr>' +
          '<td class="text-center fw-bold">' + esc(m.urutan) + '</td>' +
          '<td>' + esc(m.namaJenisKegiatan) + '</td>' +
          '<td class="text-end fw-bold px-3">' + count + ' Kegiatan</td>' +
          '<td class="text-end px-3 text-muted">' + pct + '%</td>' +
        '</tr>';
      });

      html +=
                '<tr class="bg-light fw-bold">' +
                  '<td colspan="2" class="text-center text-uppercase">TOTAL KESELURUHAN KEGIATAN TERCATAT</td>' +
                  '<td class="text-end text-primary px-3 fs-6">' + grandTotal + ' Kegiatan</td>' +
                  '<td class="text-end px-3 fs-6">100%</td>' +
                '</tr>' +
              '</tbody>' +
            '</table>' +
          '</div>' +
          '</div>' +

          uraianDocHtml +

          '<div class="my-4 text-dark">' +
            '<h6 class="fw-bold text-dark border-bottom pb-1 mb-2" style="font-size:1rem;"><i class="fa-solid fa-file-contract text-primary me-2"></i>IV. PENUTUP</h6>' +
            '<p class="small text-secondary mb-2" style="text-align: justify; line-height: 1.6;">' +
              'Demikian Laporan Rekapitulasi Kegiatan Kepegawaian ini dibuat berdasarkan data aktual yang terverifikasi dalam sistem SIAMP Pemerintah Kabupaten Pasuruan.' +
            '</p>' +
          '</div>' +

          '<div class="row mt-5 pt-3 align-items-end text-center text-md-end">' +
            '<div class="col-md-6 offset-md-6">' +
              '<p class="mb-1 small">' + esc(penandatanganData.kota || 'Pasuruan') + ', ' + todayFormatted + '</p>' +
              '<p class="fw-semibold small mb-5">' + esc(penandatanganData.jabatanPejabat || 'Kepala BKPSDM Kabupaten Pasuruan') + '</p>' +
              '<div class="my-4"></div>' +
              '<h6 class="fw-bold text-decoration-underline text-dark mb-0">' + esc(penandatanganData.namaPejabat || 'NINUK IDA RUKMI, S.H., M.Si.') + '</h6>' +
              (penandatanganData.pangkatGol ? '<p class="small text-dark mb-0">' + esc(penandatanganData.pangkatGol) + '</p>' : '') +
              '<p class="small text-muted mb-0">NIP. ' + esc(penandatanganData.nipPejabat || '197005141996032001') + '</p>' +
            '</div>' +
          '</div>' +

        '</div>';

      container.innerHTML = html;

      setTimeout(function() {
        renderDokumenReportChart(countMap);
      }, 50);
    }

    function renderDokumenReportChart(countMap) {
      var ctx = document.getElementById('docReportChart');
      if (!ctx) return;

      if (docReportChartInstance) {
        docReportChartInstance.destroy();
      }

      var sortedMaster = masterKegiatanList.slice().sort(function(a,b){ return a.urutan - b.urutan; });
      var labels = sortedMaster.map(function(m){ return 'K' + String(m.urutan).padStart(2, '0'); });
      var data = sortedMaster.map(function(m){ return countMap[m.namaJenisKegiatan] || 0; });

      docReportChartInstance = new Chart(ctx, {
        type: 'bar',
        data: {
          labels: labels,
          datasets: [{
            label: 'Jumlah Transaksi Kegiatan',
            data: data,
            backgroundColor: '#1e3a8a',
            borderRadius: 4
          }]
        },
        options: {
          responsive: true,
          maintainAspectRatio: false,
          animation: false,
          plugins: {
            legend: { display: true, position: 'top' }
          },
          scales: {
            y: { beginAtZero: true, ticks: { stepSize: 1 } },
            x: { ticks: { font: { size: 9 } } }
          }
        }
      });
    }

    function downloadDokumenPDF() {
      var element = document.getElementById('dokumenLaporanContainer');
      if (!element) return;
      
      showFullPageLoading('Membentuk file PDF Laporan SIAMP...');

      var opt = {
        margin:       [8, 8, 8, 8],
        filename:     'Laporan_Kepegawaian_SIAMP_' + new Date().toISOString().substring(0,10) + '.pdf',
        image:        { type: 'jpeg', quality: 0.98 },
        html2canvas:  { scale: 2, useCORS: true, logging: false },
        jsPDF:        { unit: 'mm', format: 'a4', orientation: 'portrait' }
      };

      if (typeof html2pdf !== 'undefined') {
        html2pdf().set(opt).from(element).save().then(function() {
          hideFullPageLoading();
          Swal.fire({
            icon: 'success',
            title: 'Unduh PDF Berhasil',
            text: 'Dokumen Laporan telah diunduh sebagai berkas PDF.',
            timer: 2000,
            showConfirmButton: false
          });
        }).catch(function(err) {
          hideFullPageLoading();
          window.print();
        });
      } else {
        hideFullPageLoading();
        window.print();
      }
    }

    function downloadDokumenWord() {
      var element = document.getElementById('dokumenLaporanContainer');
      if (!element) return;

      showFullPageLoading('Membentuk file Word (.doc)...');

      setTimeout(function() {
        var header = "<html xmlns:o='urn:schemas-microsoft-com:office:office' " +
            "xmlns:w='urn:schemas-microsoft-com:office:word' " +
            "xmlns='http://www.w3.org/TR/REC-html40'>" +
            "<head><meta charset='utf-8'><title>Dokumen Laporan SIAMP</title>" +
            "<style>body{font-family:'Times New Roman', serif; font-size:11pt;} table{border-collapse:collapse;width:100%;} th,td{border:1px solid #000;padding:5px;}</style>" +
            "</head><body>";
        var footer = "</body></html>";
        var htmlContent = header + element.innerHTML + footer;

        var blob = new Blob(['\ufeff', htmlContent], {
          type: 'application/msword'
        });

        var fileName = 'Laporan_Kepegawaian_SIAMP_' + new Date().toISOString().substring(0,10) + '.doc';

        var downloadLink = document.createElement("a");
        document.body.appendChild(downloadLink);

        if (navigator.msSaveOrOpenBlob) {
          navigator.msSaveOrOpenBlob(blob, fileName);
        } else {
          downloadLink.href = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
          downloadLink.download = fileName;
          downloadLink.click();
        }
        document.body.removeChild(downloadLink);

        hideFullPageLoading();
        Swal.fire({
          icon: 'success',
          title: 'Unduh Word Berhasil',
          text: 'Dokumen Laporan berhasil diunduh dalam format Microsoft Word (.doc).',
          timer: 2000,
          showConfirmButton: false
        });
      }, 300);
    }

    function openInlinePenandatanganModal() {
      document.getElementById('inline_sign_nama').value = penandatanganData.namaPejabat || '';
      document.getElementById('inline_sign_nip').value = penandatanganData.nipPejabat || '';
      document.getElementById('inline_sign_pangkat').value = penandatanganData.pangkatGol || '';
      document.getElementById('inline_sign_jabatan').value = penandatanganData.jabatanPejabat || '';
      document.getElementById('inline_sign_instansi').value = penandatanganData.instansi || '';
      document.getElementById('inline_sign_kota').value = penandatanganData.kota || 'Pasuruan';
      new bootstrap.Modal(document.getElementById('inlinePenandatanganModal')).show();
    }

    function submitInlinePenandatanganForm() {
      penandatanganData = {
        namaPejabat: document.getElementById('inline_sign_nama').value.trim(),
        nipPejabat: document.getElementById('inline_sign_nip').value.trim(),
        pangkatGol: document.getElementById('inline_sign_pangkat').value.trim(),
        jabatanPejabat: document.getElementById('inline_sign_jabatan').value.trim(),
        instansi: document.getElementById('inline_sign_instansi').value.trim(),
        kota: document.getElementById('inline_sign_kota').value.trim()
      };

      var modalEl = document.getElementById('inlinePenandatanganModal');
      var modalInst = bootstrap.Modal.getInstance(modalEl);
      if (modalInst) modalInst.hide();

      renderDokumenLaporanView();
      if (typeof google !== 'undefined') gasRun('savePenandatangan', penandatanganData);
      Swal.fire({ icon: 'success', title: 'Pejabat Penandatangan Diperbarui', toast: true, position: 'top-end', showConfirmButton: false, timer: 1500 });
    }

    function renderUsersTable() {
      var container = document.getElementById('usersTableContainer');
      if (!container) return;

      if (userDB.length === 0) {
        container.innerHTML = '<div class="text-center py-5"><p class="text-muted">Belum ada akun pengguna.</p></div>';
        return;
      }

      var html = '<div class="table-responsive"><table class="table table-custom table-hover align-middle mb-0"><thead><tr><th>User ID</th><th>Username & Nama Lengkap</th><th>Role</th><th>Hak Akses Kegiatan</th><th>Status</th><th class="text-end">Aksi</th></tr></thead><tbody>';

      userDB.forEach(function(u) {
        var roleBadge = (u.role === 'Admin')
          ? '<span class="badge bg-primary px-2.5 py-1">Admin (Penuh)</span>'
          : '<span class="badge bg-secondary px-2.5 py-1">User (Terbatas)</span>';

        var statusBadge = (u.status === 'Aktif')
          ? '<span class="badge bg-success-subtle text-success border">Aktif</span>'
          : '<span class="badge bg-danger-subtle text-danger border">Nonaktif</span>';

        var allowedText = u.allowedActivities === 'ALL' ? 'Semua 40 Jenis Kegiatan' : (u.allowedActivities ? u.allowedActivities.split(',').length + ' Jenis Kegiatan' : '-');

        html += '<tr>' +
          '<td><code>' + esc(u.userID) + '</code></td>' +
          '<td><div><strong class="text-dark d-block">' + esc(u.username) + '</strong><small class="text-muted">' + esc(u.fullname || '-') + '</small></div></td>' +
          '<td>' + roleBadge + '</td>' +
          '<td><small class="text-dark">' + allowedText + '</small></td>' +
          '<td>' + statusBadge + '</td>' +
          '<td class="text-end">' +
            '<button class="btn btn-sm btn-light border text-primary me-1" onclick="editUser(\'' + escJs(u.userID) + '\')"><i class="fa-solid fa-pen-to-square"></i> Edit</button>' +
            '<button class="btn btn-sm btn-light border text-danger" onclick="deleteUserAction(\'' + escJs(u.userID) + '\')"><i class="fa-solid fa-trash"></i> Hapus</button>' +
          '</td>' +
        '</tr>';
      });

      html += '</tbody></table></div>';
      container.innerHTML = html;
    }

    function toggleUserRoleActivities() {
      var role = document.getElementById('modal_user_role').value;
      var wrap = document.getElementById('wrapper_user_activities');
      if (wrap) wrap.style.display = (role === 'User') ? '' : 'none';
    }

    function toggleAllActivityCheckboxes(chkAll) {
      document.querySelectorAll('.user-act-chk').forEach(function(c) {
        c.checked = chkAll.checked;
      });
    }

    function openUserModal(userID) {
      document.getElementById('userForm').reset();
      document.getElementById('modal_user_id').value = '';

      var checklist = document.getElementById('userActivityChecklist');
      var sortedMaster = masterKegiatanList.slice().sort(function(a,b){ return a.urutan - b.urutan; });
      var chkHtml = '';

      sortedMaster.forEach(function(m) {
        chkHtml += '<div class="col-md-6"><div class="form-check"><input class="form-check-input user-act-chk" type="checkbox" value="' + esc(m.jenisKegiatanID) + '" id="chk_act_' + esc(m.jenisKegiatanID) + '"><label class="form-check-label small" for="chk_act_' + esc(m.jenisKegiatanID) + '">' + esc(m.urutan) + '. ' + esc(m.namaJenisKegiatan) + '</label></div></div>';
      });
      checklist.innerHTML = chkHtml;

      var modalTitle = document.getElementById('modalUserTitle');

      if (userID) {
        var u = userDB.find(function(x){ return x.userID === userID; });
        if (!u) return;
        modalTitle.innerHTML = '<i class="fa-solid fa-user-pen text-primary me-2"></i>Edit Pengguna System';
        document.getElementById('modal_user_id').value = u.userID;
        document.getElementById('modal_user_username').value = u.username;
        document.getElementById('modal_user_fullname').value = u.fullname;
        document.getElementById('modal_user_password').value = '';
        document.getElementById('modal_user_role').value = u.role || 'User';
        document.getElementById('modal_user_status').value = u.status || 'Aktif';

        var allowedList = (u.allowedActivities || 'ALL').split(',').map(function(s){ return s.trim(); });
        var isAll = u.allowedActivities === 'ALL';
        document.getElementById('user_act_all').checked = isAll;

        document.querySelectorAll('.user-act-chk').forEach(function(chk) {
          chk.checked = isAll || allowedList.includes(chk.value);
        });
      } else {
        modalTitle.innerHTML = '<i class="fa-solid fa-user-plus text-primary me-2"></i>Tambah Pengguna System Baru';
        document.getElementById('modal_user_role').value = 'User';
        document.getElementById('user_act_all').checked = true;
        document.querySelectorAll('.user-act-chk').forEach(function(chk) { chk.checked = true; });
      }

      toggleUserRoleActivities();
      new bootstrap.Modal(document.getElementById('userModal')).show();
    }

    function editUser(userID) {
      openUserModal(userID);
    }

    function submitUserForm() {
      var userID = document.getElementById('modal_user_id').value;
      var username = document.getElementById('modal_user_username').value.trim();
      var fullname = document.getElementById('modal_user_fullname').value.trim();
      var pass = document.getElementById('modal_user_password').value.trim();
      var role = document.getElementById('modal_user_role').value;
      var status = document.getElementById('modal_user_status').value;

      if (!username || !fullname) return Swal.fire('Peringatan', 'Username & Nama Lengkap wajib diisi!', 'warning');

      var allowedStr = 'ALL';
      if (role === 'User') {
        var selected = [];
        document.querySelectorAll('.user-act-chk:checked').forEach(function(c) { selected.push(c.value); });
        if (selected.length === 0) return Swal.fire('Peringatan', 'Pilih minimal satu hak akses kegiatan untuk User!', 'warning');
        allowedStr = selected.join(',');
      }

      var payload = {
        userID: userID,
        username: username,
        fullname: fullname,
        password: pass,
        role: role,
        allowedActivities: allowedStr,
        status: status
      };

      var modalEl = document.getElementById('userModal');
      var modalInst = bootstrap.Modal.getInstance(modalEl);
      if (modalInst) modalInst.hide();

      Swal.fire({ title: 'Menyimpan pengguna...', allowOutsideClick: false, didOpen: function() { Swal.showLoading(); } });

      if (typeof google !== 'undefined') {
        gasRun('saveUser', payload).then(function(res) {
          Swal.close();
          if (res.success) {
            fetchUsersData(true);
            Swal.fire('Berhasil', res.message, 'success');
          } else Swal.fire('Gagal', res.message, 'error');
        }).catch(function(err) { Swal.close(); Swal.fire('Error', err.message, 'error'); });
      } else {
        setTimeout(function() {
          Swal.close();
          var tempID = userID || ('USR-' + Date.now());
          var item = Object.assign({}, payload, { userID: tempID });
          var idx = userDB.findIndex(function(x){ return x.userID === userID; });
          if (idx !== -1) userDB[idx] = item;
          else userDB.push(item);
          renderUsersTable();
          Swal.fire('Berhasil (Simulasi)', 'Data pengguna berhasil disimpan.', 'success');
        }, 600);
      }
    }

    function deleteUserAction(userID) {
      Swal.fire({ title: 'Hapus Pengguna?', text: 'Pengguna ID ' + userID + ' akan dihapus.', icon: 'warning', showCancelButton: true, confirmButtonColor: '#d33', confirmButtonText: 'Ya, Hapus' }).then(function(res) {
        if (!res.isConfirmed) return;

        if (typeof google !== 'undefined') {
          gasRun('deleteUser', userID).then(function(r) {
            if (r.success) fetchUsersData(true);
            else Swal.fire('Error', r.message, 'error');
          });
        } else {
          userDB = userDB.filter(function(x){ return x.userID !== userID; });
          renderUsersTable();
          Swal.fire('Sukses', 'Pengguna berhasil dihapus (Simulasi)', 'success');
        }
      });
    }

    function restoreMasterBakuAction() {
      Swal.fire({
        title: 'Pulihkan master K01-K40', icon: 'question', showCancelButton: true, showDenyButton: true,
        html: '<div class="text-start small"><b>Tambah yang hilang</b>: hanya menambahkan ID K01-K40 yang belum ada.<br><b>Samakan dengan baku</b>: juga mengembalikan nama, urutan, dan status K01-K40 ke daftar baku (perubahan nama yang pernah Anda buat akan tertimpa).</div>',
        confirmButtonText: 'Tambah yang hilang', denyButtonText: 'Samakan dengan baku', cancelButtonText: 'Batal'
      }).then(function(r) {
        if (!r.isConfirmed && !r.isDenied) return;
        var samakan = !!r.isDenied;
        if (typeof google === 'undefined') {
          mockMasterList.forEach(function(m) {
            var ex = masterKegiatanList.find(function(x) { return x.jenisKegiatanID === m.jenisKegiatanID; });
            if (!ex) masterKegiatanList.push(Object.assign({}, m));
            else if (samakan) Object.assign(ex, m);
          });
          populateMasterDropdowns(); renderMasterTable();
          Swal.fire({ icon: 'success', title: 'Master dipulihkan (Simulasi)', toast: true, position: 'top-end', showConfirmButton: false, timer: 1600 });
          return;
        }
        showSectionLoading('masterTableContainer');
        gasRun('restoreMasterBaku', samakan).then(function(res) {
          return fetchMasterData().then(function() { renderMasterTable(); Swal.fire({ icon: 'success', title: 'Berhasil', text: res.message, timer: 2600, showConfirmButton: false }); });
        }).catch(function(err) { renderMasterTable(); Swal.fire('Error', err.message, 'error'); });
      });
    }

    function renderMasterTable() {
      var container = document.getElementById('masterTableContainer');
      if (!container) return;

      if (!masterKegiatanList.length) {
        container.innerHTML = '<div class="text-center py-5"><i class="fa-solid fa-list-check fa-3x text-muted mb-3 opacity-50"></i><p class="text-muted mb-3">Master jenis kegiatan belum tersedia.</p><button class="btn btn-primary btn-sm" onclick="restoreMasterBakuAction()"><i class="fa-solid fa-rotate-left me-1"></i> Pulihkan K01-K40</button></div>';
        return;
      }

      var sortedMaster = masterKegiatanList.slice().sort(function(a,b){ return a.urutan - b.urutan; });
      var stdName = {}; mockMasterList.forEach(function(m) { stdName[m.jenisKegiatanID] = m.namaJenisKegiatan; });
      sortedMaster.forEach(function(m) { if (stdName[m.jenisKegiatanID] && (!String(m.namaJenisKegiatan || '').trim() || /^K\d{1,3}$/i.test(String(m.namaJenisKegiatan).trim()))) m.namaJenisKegiatan = stdName[m.jenisKegiatanID]; });

      var html = '<div class="table-responsive"><table class="table table-custom table-hover align-middle mb-0"><thead><tr><th style="width:70px;">ID</th><th style="width:80px;">Urutan</th><th>Nama Jenis Kegiatan Baku</th><th>Status</th><th class="text-end">Aksi</th></tr></thead><tbody>';

      sortedMaster.forEach(function(m) {
        var statusBadge = (m.status === 'Aktif')
          ? '<span class="badge bg-success-subtle text-success border">Aktif</span>'
          : '<span class="badge bg-secondary-subtle text-secondary border">Nonaktif</span>';

        html += '<tr>' +
          '<td><code>' + esc(m.jenisKegiatanID) + '</code></td>' +
          '<td><span class="badge bg-light text-dark border fw-bold">' + esc(m.urutan) + '</span></td>' +
          '<td><strong class="text-dark">' + esc(m.namaJenisKegiatan) + '</strong></td>' +
          '<td>' + statusBadge + '</td>' +
          '<td class="text-end">' +
            '<button class="btn btn-sm btn-light border text-primary me-1" onclick="editMaster(\'' + escJs(m.jenisKegiatanID) + '\')"><i class="fa-solid fa-pen-to-square"></i> Edit</button>' +
            '<button class="btn btn-sm btn-light border text-danger" onclick="deleteMasterAction(\'' + escJs(m.jenisKegiatanID) + '\')"><i class="fa-solid fa-trash"></i> Hapus</button>' +
          '</td>' +
        '</tr>';
      });

      html += '</tbody></table></div>';
      container.innerHTML = html;
    }

    function openMasterModal(jenisKegiatanID) {
      document.getElementById('masterForm').reset();
      document.getElementById('modal_master_id').value = '';

      var modalTitle = document.getElementById('modalMasterTitle');

      if (jenisKegiatanID) {
        var m = masterKegiatanList.find(function(x){ return x.jenisKegiatanID === jenisKegiatanID; });
        if (!m) return;
        modalTitle.innerHTML = '<i class="fa-solid fa-pen-to-square text-primary me-2"></i>Edit Master Jenis Kegiatan';
        document.getElementById('modal_master_id').value = m.jenisKegiatanID;
        document.getElementById('modal_master_nama').value = m.namaJenisKegiatan;
        document.getElementById('modal_master_urutan').value = m.urutan;
        document.getElementById('modal_master_status').value = m.status || 'Aktif';
      } else {
        modalTitle.innerHTML = '<i class="fa-solid fa-plus text-primary me-2"></i>Tambah Master Jenis Kegiatan';
        document.getElementById('modal_master_urutan').value = masterKegiatanList.length + 1;
        document.getElementById('modal_master_status').value = 'Aktif';
      }

      new bootstrap.Modal(document.getElementById('masterModal')).show();
    }

    function editMaster(jenisKegiatanID) {
      openMasterModal(jenisKegiatanID);
    }

    function submitMasterForm() {
      var id = document.getElementById('modal_master_id').value;
      var nama = document.getElementById('modal_master_nama').value.trim();
      var urutan = document.getElementById('modal_master_urutan').value;
      var status = document.getElementById('modal_master_status').value;

      if (!nama || !urutan) return Swal.fire('Peringatan', 'Nama Jenis Kegiatan & Urutan wajib diisi!', 'warning');

      var payload = { jenisKegiatanID: id, namaJenisKegiatan: nama, urutan: urutan, status: status };

      var modalEl = document.getElementById('masterModal');
      var modalInst = bootstrap.Modal.getInstance(modalEl);
      if (modalInst) modalInst.hide();

      if (typeof google !== 'undefined') {
        gasRun('saveMasterJenisKegiatan', payload).then(function(res) {
          if (res.success) {
            fetchMasterData().then(function(){ renderMasterTable(); });
            Swal.fire('Berhasil', res.message, 'success');
          } else Swal.fire('Error', res.message, 'error');
        });
      } else {
        var tempID = id || ('K' + String(masterKegiatanList.length + 1).padStart(2, '0'));
        var idx = masterKegiatanList.findIndex(function(x){ return x.jenisKegiatanID === id; });
        var item = Object.assign({}, payload, { jenisKegiatanID: tempID, urutan: Number(urutan) });
        if (idx !== -1) masterKegiatanList[idx] = item;
        else masterKegiatanList.push(item);

        populateMasterDropdowns();
        renderMasterTable();
        Swal.fire('Sukses', 'Master kegiatan berhasil disimpan (Simulasi)', 'success');
      }
    }

    function deleteMasterAction(jenisKegiatanID) {
      Swal.fire({ title: 'Hapus Master Kegiatan?', text: 'Master ID ' + jenisKegiatanID + ' akan dihapus.', icon: 'warning', showCancelButton: true, confirmButtonColor: '#d33', confirmButtonText: 'Ya, Hapus' }).then(function(res) {
        if (!res.isConfirmed) return;

        if (typeof google !== 'undefined') {
          gasRun('deleteMasterJenisKegiatan', jenisKegiatanID).then(function(r) {
            if (r.success) fetchMasterData().then(function(){ renderMasterTable(); });
            else Swal.fire('Error', r.message, 'error');
          });
        } else {
          masterKegiatanList = masterKegiatanList.filter(function(x){ return x.jenisKegiatanID !== jenisKegiatanID; });
          populateMasterDropdowns();
          renderMasterTable();
          Swal.fire('Sukses', 'Master kegiatan dihapus (Simulasi)', 'success');
        }
      });
    }

    function initPenandatanganForm() {
      document.getElementById('sign_nama').value = penandatanganData.namaPejabat || '';
      document.getElementById('sign_nip').value = penandatanganData.nipPejabat || '';
      document.getElementById('sign_pangkat').value = penandatanganData.pangkatGol || '';
      document.getElementById('sign_jabatan').value = penandatanganData.jabatanPejabat || '';
      document.getElementById('sign_instansi').value = penandatanganData.instansi || '';
      document.getElementById('sign_kota').value = penandatanganData.kota || 'Pasuruan';
    }

    function submitPenandatanganForm() {
      var payload = {
        namaPejabat: document.getElementById('sign_nama').value.trim(),
        nipPejabat: document.getElementById('sign_nip').value.trim(),
        pangkatGol: document.getElementById('sign_pangkat').value.trim(),
        jabatanPejabat: document.getElementById('sign_jabatan').value.trim(),
        instansi: document.getElementById('sign_instansi').value.trim(),
        kota: document.getElementById('sign_kota').value.trim()
      };

      if (!payload.namaPejabat || !payload.nipPejabat || !payload.jabatanPejabat) {
        return Swal.fire('Peringatan', 'Harap isi semua bidang wajib!', 'warning');
      }

      Swal.fire({ title: 'Menyimpan Penandatangan...', allowOutsideClick: false, didOpen: function() { Swal.showLoading(); } });

      if (typeof google !== 'undefined') {
        gasRun('savePenandatangan', payload).then(function(res) {
          Swal.close();
          if (res.success) {
            penandatanganData = payload;
            if (activePage === 'dokumen') renderDokumenLaporanView();
            Swal.fire('Berhasil', res.message, 'success');
          } else Swal.fire('Gagal', res.message, 'error');
        }).catch(function(err) { Swal.close(); Swal.fire('Error', err.message, 'error'); });
      } else {
        setTimeout(function() {
          Swal.close();
          penandatanganData = payload;
          if (activePage === 'dokumen') renderDokumenLaporanView();
          Swal.fire('Berhasil (Simulasi)', 'Data penandatangan laporan berhasil disimpan.', 'success');
        }, 600);
      }
    }

    function initAccountSettingsForm() {
      if (!currentUser) return;
      document.getElementById('acc_username').value = currentUser.username || 'admin';
      document.getElementById('acc_old_password').value = '';
      document.getElementById('acc_new_password').value = '';
      document.getElementById('acc_confirm_password').value = '';
    }

    function submitAccountSettings() {
      if (!currentUser) return;
      var newUsername = document.getElementById('acc_username').value.trim();
      var oldPass = document.getElementById('acc_old_password').value.trim();
      var newPass = document.getElementById('acc_new_password').value.trim();
      var confirmPass = document.getElementById('acc_confirm_password').value.trim();

      if (!newUsername) return Swal.fire('Peringatan', 'Username wajib diisi!', 'warning');

      if (newPass || oldPass) {
        if (!oldPass) return Swal.fire('Peringatan', 'Masukkan Password saat ini (lama)!', 'warning');
        if (!newPass) return Swal.fire('Peringatan', 'Masukkan Password Baru!', 'warning');
        if (newPass !== confirmPass) return Swal.fire('Peringatan', 'Konfirmasi Password Baru tidak cocok!', 'warning');
      }

      var payload = {
        currentUsername: currentUser.username,
        newUsername: newUsername,
        oldPassword: oldPass,
        newPassword: newPass
      };

      Swal.fire({ title: 'Menyimpan Pengaturan Akun...', allowOutsideClick: false, didOpen: function() { Swal.showLoading(); } });

      if (typeof google !== 'undefined') {
        gasRun('updateAccount', payload).then(function(res) {
          Swal.close();
          if (res.success) {
            currentUser.username = res.newUsername;
            document.getElementById('displayUser').innerText = currentUser.fullname || currentUser.username;
            document.getElementById('acc_old_password').value = '';
            document.getElementById('acc_new_password').value = '';
            document.getElementById('acc_confirm_password').value = '';
            Swal.fire('Berhasil', res.message, 'success');
          } else {
            Swal.fire('Gagal', res.message, 'error');
          }
        }).catch(function(err) { Swal.close(); Swal.fire('Error', err.message, 'error'); });
      } else {
        setTimeout(function() {
          Swal.close();
          currentUser.username = newUsername;
          document.getElementById('displayUser').innerText = currentUser.fullname || currentUser.username;
          Swal.fire('Berhasil (Simulasi)', 'Username dan password berhasil diperbarui.', 'success');
        }, 800);
      }
    }

    document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function() {
      if (typeof google === 'undefined') {
        var previewInfo = document.getElementById('previewLoginInfo');
        if (previewInfo) previewInfo.classList.remove('d-none');
        var badge = document.createElement('div');
        badge.className = 'position-fixed bottom-0 start-0 m-3 badge bg-warning text-dark shadow px-3 py-2 no-print';
        badge.style.zIndex = '9998';
        badge.innerHTML = '⚠️ MODE PREVIEW (BKPSDM KAB. PASURUAN)';
        document.body.appendChild(badge);
      }
      else {
        var uEl = document.getElementById('username'), pEl = document.getElementById('password');
        if (uEl) uEl.value = '';
        if (pEl) pEl.value = '';
      }
      applyLogo();
      startClock();
      checkLoginState();
    });
  </script>
